Result: 在ACDC和4D-Lung数据集上的实验表明，该方法优于现有基线方法，能够从稀疏观测中生成解剖学合理且连贯的中间体积，达到最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 提出的相位结构先验连续形变方法能有效处理4D医学图像中的非均匀和近周期运动，实现高质量的高保真插值结果。

Abstract: 4D medical image interpolation aims to recover missing volumes from sparsely observed time points and is important for dynamic anatomical analysis in applications such as cardiac MRI and thoracic CT, where motion is often repetitive or near-periodic over clinically relevant intervals. A key challenge is that this structure is not always encoded directly in deformation representations for interpolation. In addition, physiological motion is often non-uniform, so equal temporal intervals do not necessarily correspond to equal amounts of anatomical change. To address these issues, we formulate interpolation as learning a continuous deformation process with a phase-structured prior. Given two endpoint volumes, we parameterize a phase-conditioned velocity field with a finite Fourier basis, which embeds near-periodic motion patterns directly into the deformation space and supports continuous querying at arbitrary target times. We further introduce a phase-aligned temporal reparameterization that maps normalized within-interval time to a latent motion phase according to deformation variation intensity, thereby better modeling non-uniform motion progression. Intermediate volumes are then synthesized by continuously warping both endpoints, followed by bidirectional fusion and lightweight residual refinement. Experiments on ACDC and 4D-Lung show that the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance over existing baselines while producing anatomically plausible and coherent intermediate volumes from sparse observations.

</details>


### [37] [ConsensusTAS: Self-Supervised Temporal Action Segmentation for Long-Horizon Construction Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24043)
*Xiaoshan Zhou,Yafei Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ConsensusTAS，一种无标签自监督学习方法，用于将连续视频流分割为不同的活动阶段，解决了长期建筑视频中动作边界标注耗时的问题。该方法在多个数据集上表现优异，且无需大型视觉-语言模型，可在CPU上运行，适用于视频监控和人机协作场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的建筑施工工人活动识别研究主要局限于分类活动类别（如攀爬、搬运），缺乏对长时间序列中细粒度活动转换的识别能力。由于在长视频中标注动作时间边界非常耗时，这一挑战阻碍了更精细的活动理解，而这对协作式人-机器人工作（如及时提供工具或支持）至关重要。

Method: 提出ConsensusTAS算法，这是一种基于内部共识利用的无标签自监督学习方法。该算法通过分析候选分割的内部一致性，将连续视频流分割为 distinct 的活动阶段，无需依赖人工标注的时间边界数据。

Result: 1. 在三个公开数据集上优于最先进方法：GTEA数据集F1@10为73.08，Breakfast数据集F1@10为64.33，Assembly101静态摄像头视频F1@50为33.50。
2. 在实际建筑视频测试中，成功识别并分割了砌砖复合活动中的细粒度动作（如抹灰、放置砖块、按压和对齐）。
3. 与需要计算密集型大型视觉-语言模型的模型相比，该方法可在CPU上运行。

Conclusion: ConsensusTAS提供了一种高效、低计算成本的动作分割解决方案，能够有效识别长时间序列中的细粒度活动转换，具有实际应用价值，特别适用于资源受限的移动机器人平台上的视频监控和人机协作任务。

Abstract: Recognizing sequential construction activities is important for collaborative human-robot work; for example, robots are able to understand workers' current and upcoming actions and provide timely tool delivery or physical support. However, despite extensive research on construction worker activity recognition, existing studies have been limited to classifying activity categories, such as climbing, lifting, and walking, instead of recognizing fine-grained activity transitions from long-horizon sequences. Addressing this problem is challenging because annotating action temporal boundaries in long construction videos is time-consuming. In this study, we propose ConsensusTAS, a label-free, self-supervised learning approach to segment continuous video streams into distinct activity phases by exploiting the internal consensus of candidate segmentations. We evaluated our algorithm on three public datasets, where it outperformed state-of-the-art methods, achieving an F1@10 of 73.08 on GTEA, an F1@10 of 64.33 on Breakfast, and an F1@50 of 33.50 on static-camera videos from Assembly101. We also tested it on real-world construction videos, where post-hoc evaluation showed that the model successfully recognized and segmented actions within the composite activity of bricklaying, such as spreading mortar on a brick, placing the brick, pressing, and aligning. Compared with other temporal action segmentation models that require computationally intensive large vision-language models, our method can run on a CPU, which provides practical value for video surveillance and human-robot collaboration on mobile robotic platforms.

</details>


### [38] [VisCache: Visual KV Cache Pruning for Efficient Vision Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24063)
*Lyuke Wang,Zhuo Li,Guangxu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisCache是一个无需训练的即插即用框架，通过粗粒度关键帧选择和细粒度PruneKV算法，在保持性能的同时显著降低VLLM长上下文推理的计算和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Vision Large Language Models (VLLMs) 在处理长上下文时因视觉Key-Value (KV) 缓存导致的巨大计算和内存开销问题；现有均匀剪枝方法导致信息丢失和性能下降。

Method: 提出VisCache框架，包含两个协同阶段：1. 使用轻量级VLM过滤时间冗余，选择语义信息丰富的关键帧（粗粒度）；2. 引入PruneKV算法，基于抛物线层预算分配和非对称更新机制（选择性剪枝keys并融合values），进行针对注意力动态的手术式KV压缩（细粒度）。

Result: 实验显示VisCache实现了最高2.35倍的加速比和显著的内存减少，仅保留19--28%的KV缓存即可维持具有竞争力的性能，且在效率与性能的Pareto前沿上优于现有基线。

Conclusion: VisCache为长上下文VLLM推理建立了一种新的效率与性能平衡范式，有效解决了视觉KV缓存的高昂成本问题。

Abstract: While Vision Large Language Models (VLLMs) have achieved remarkable success in multimodal reasoning, their long-context inference remains prohibitively expensive due to the massive computation and memory overhead of visual Key-Value (KV) caches. Existing KV compression methods often apply uniform pruning across visual tokens and layers, leading to substantial information loss and degraded performance.To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{VisCache}, a plug-and-play framework for coarse-to-fine \textbf{Vis}ual KV \textbf{Cache} pruning without training, which consists of two synergistic stages. First, a lightweight VLM filters temporal redundancy by selectively forwarding semantically informative keyframes. Second, we introduce {PruneKV}, a surgical KV compression algorithm tailored to the attention dynamics of VLLMs. Unlike rigid pruning strategies, PruneKV adopts a parabolic layer-wise budget allocation together with an asymmetric update mechanism that selectively prunes keys while fusing values, thereby preserving critical contextual information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VisCache substantially improves inference efficiency, achieving up to {2.35$\times$ speedup} and significant memory reduction while maintaining competitive performance with only {19--28\%} KV cache retention. VisCache consistently outperforms existing baselines, establishing a new Pareto frontier between efficiency and performance for long-context VLLM inference. Code is available at https://github.com/Wlklk/VisCache

</details>


### [39] [Representation Learning in Diffusion and Flow-based Model: An Application Aspect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24068)
*Yanchen Xu,Sida Huang,Zhenyu Gu,Ruishu Zhu,Yilan Gao,Hongyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了扩散模型和基于流的模型在生成建模中的主导地位，及其与表示学习的双向关系。提出了一个三层渐进式框架，系统梳理了利用表示学习提升生成能力、利用生成模型提取感知表示以及迈向通用统一应用的研究进展，并分类了下游任务方法，旨在为研究者提供参考和未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和流模型因能通过大规模训练学习丰富的多级视觉表征而成为生成建模的主导范式，形成了生成模型与表示学习之间的双向促进关系。现有研究缺乏对这一互动关系的系统性梳理，亟需明确其内在逻辑并指导未来探索。

Method: 提出一个三层渐进式框架，从三个视角组织现有工作：1) 使用表示学习改进生成能力；2) 利用生成模型提取用于感知任务的表示；3) 迈向通用统一应用。对图像分类、密集视觉预测、实例级感知及标注稀缺场景等下游任务中的代表性方法进行系统分类和统一分类法构建。

Result: 成功建立了生成模型与表示学习互动的统一分类体系，系统化了从基础生成到感知任务再到通用应用的演进路径，明确了各类下游任务中代表性方法的归属与挑战。

Conclusion: 该综述澄清了当前研究的底层逻辑，指出了关键挑战，并为利用生成模型的表示能力拓展至生成之外应用的研究提供了有价值的参考和前景方向。

Abstract: Diffusion models and flow-based models have recently become the dominant paradigms in generative modeling, largely due to their ability to learn rich, multi-level visual representations through large-scale training. This creates a bidirectional relationship between generative models and representation learning: improving representation learning enhances generation quality, while the learned representations can be leveraged for broader understanding tasks. This survey systematically explores this interplay with a focus on applications. We propose a three-tier progressive framework that organizes existing works from three perspectives: using representation learning to improve generative capabilities, exploiting generative models to extract representations for perception tasks, and ultimately moving toward general-purpose unified applications. We systematically categorize representative methods across a wide range of downstream tasks, including image classification, dense visual prediction, instance-level perception, and annotation-scarce scenarios. By providing a unified taxonomy and identifying key challenges, this survey aims to clarify the underlying logic of current research and suggest promising directions for future exploration. We hope this work can serve as a valuable reference for researchers interested in harnessing the representation power of generative models for applications beyond generation.

</details>


### [40] [Joint-Embedding Prediction of Masked Point Tubes for Self-Supervised Learning on 4D Point Cloud Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24093)
*Jheng-Ling Lee,Shang-Tse Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于JEPA风格的4D点云视频自监督学习框架，通过潜在点管预测替代传统重建，结合各向同性高斯正则化，有效捕捉时空结构并提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决4D点云视频自监督学习中标注成本高以及基于重建的预训练容易过度强调低层几何细节的问题。

Method: 构建JEPA风格框架，对时空区域进行掩码并在特征空间中从可见上下文预测目标表示；引入Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularization以稳定潜在预测，防止嵌入坍塌，无需显式重建目标。

Result: 在动作和手势识别基准测试中，所学表示提升了下游微调、少样本学习和跨数据集迁移的性能。

Conclusion: JEPA风格的潜在预测是4D点云视频预训练的一种有前景的替代方案，优于以重建为中心的方法。

Abstract: Self-supervised representation learning for 4D point cloud videos is challenging because annotations are costly and reconstruction-based pretraining can overemphasize low-level geometric details. We propose a JEPA-style framework that learns from unlabeled spatiotemporal point clouds through latent point-tube prediction. Instead of reconstructing raw coordinates, the model masks spatiotemporal regions and predicts their target representations from visible context representations in feature space. To stabilize latent prediction, we incorporate Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularization, which encourages non-collapsed embeddings without relying on explicit reconstruction targets. This formulation aims to capture both spatial structure and temporal dynamics while keeping the pretraining objective aligned with downstream semantic recognition. Experiments on action and gesture recognition benchmarks show that the learned representations improve downstream fine-tuning, limited-label learning, and cross-dataset transfer. These results suggest that JEPA-style latent prediction is a promising alternative to reconstruction-centered pretraining for 4D point cloud videos.

</details>


### [41] [DRRG: A Discrete Diffusion Framework for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24105)
*Shaoyang Zhoua,Yingshu Li,Yunyi Liu,Lijun Pu,Lingqiao Liu,Lei Wang,Luping Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散扩散大语言模型（DLLM）的自动放射学报告生成框架DRRG。该框架通过迭代去噪而非传统的自回归方式，实现了报告的迭代优化和双向修正，有效解决了传统方法中的错误传播问题，并在多个数据集上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动放射学报告生成（RRG）主要依赖自回归（AR）框架，逐词生成且无法修改早期内容，导致容易出错且不符合放射科医生迭代完善报告的临床流程。尽管离散扩散大语言模型（DLLMs）具备通过迭代去噪实现文本优化的潜力，但在RRG领域尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 开发了DRRG框架，将RRG建模为迭代掩码标记去噪任务。具体包括：1) 引入临床实体感知互补掩码，以提高标记监督覆盖率并强调临床重要实体；2) 集成概念条件模块，将图像衍生的临床概念注入视觉表示中。在MIMIC-CXR和CheXpert Plus数据集上进行训练和评估。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR数据集上，DRRG在BLEU-4、CheXpert-F1、RadGraph-F1、GREEN和RaTEScore等多个指标上均优于对比方法，且使用的LLM解码器规模显著更小。在CheXpert Plus数据集上，DRRG获得了最高的BLEU-4 (0.119) 和 CheXpert-F1 (0.347)。

Conclusion: 离散扩散为放射学报告生成提供了一种有效的自回归替代方案，支持迭代的、双向的报告精炼。结合临床聚焦的掩码策略和基于图像的概念条件注入，能够显著提升报告质量和临床一致性。

Abstract: Purpose: Automatic radiology report generation (RRG) has been widely explored to improve reporting accuracy and reduce radiologists' workload. Most existing methods rely on autoregressive (AR) frameworks that generate reports token by token and cannot revise earlier content, making them prone to error propagation and inconsistent with the iterative refinement process of radiological reporting. In contrast, discrete diffusion large language models (DLLMs) generate text through iterative denoising, naturally enabling report refinement. However, DLLMs have not been extensively investigated for RRG. In this study, we developed and evaluated a discrete diffusion framework for RRG that enables iterative refinement rather than conventional left-to-right autoregressive decoding.
  Materials and methods: We developed DRRG, a DLLM-based framework that formulates RRG as iterative masked-token denoising. DRRG incorporates a clinical-entities-aware complementary mask to improve token supervision coverage and emphasize clinically important entities, together with a concept-conditioning module that injects image-derived clinical concepts into visual representations. DRRG was trained and evaluated on MIMIC-CXR and CheXpert Plus.
  Results: On MIMIC-CXR, DRRG achieved BLEU-4 of 0.210, CheXpert-F1 of 0.549, RadGraph-F1 of 0.281, GREEN of 0.360, and RaTEScore of 0.604, outperforming the compared methods on most reported metrics, despite employing a substantially smaller LLM decoder. On CheXpert Plus, DRRG achieved the highest BLEU-4 (0.119) and CheXpert-F1 (0.347) among the compared methods.
  Conclusion: Discrete diffusion provides an effective alternative to autoregressive radiology report generation by enabling iterative, bidirectional report refinement. Incorporating clinically focused masking and image-derived concept conditioning improves report quality and clinical consistency.

</details>


### [42] [MatReplace: A Reference-Free, Conditioning-Aligned Benchmark for Material Replacement in Interior Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24107)
*Mingzhe Du,Thong Thanh Nguyen,Nguyen Tran Cong Duy,See-Kiong Ng,Luu Anh Tuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MatReplace基准，用于评估室内设计中材料替换任务的性能。该基准无需参考图像，从局部材质正确性、全局光照和谐、外部区域保留和内部结构四个维度进行评估，并揭示了命名渲染与视觉 grounding 之间的显著差异：最强模型已能较好完成基于名称的材质渲染，但基于像素/图像的视觉 grounding 仍是开放难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有材料替换任务缺乏公开基准，且传统基于参考的指标在一对多生成场景中存在偏差（惩罚有效输出、偏向参考生成器风格），无法公平比较不同引导形式的编辑模型。

Method: 引入MatReplace基准，包含三个赛道以变化单一条件信号：(A)仅指令，(B)指令加区域掩码，(C)材质参考图像替代指令。评估维度包括局部材质正确性、全局光照和谐、外部区域保留和内部结构。

Result: 1. Track A中，领先闭源编辑器实现示例级材质渲染，表现优于示例锚点；2. Track B中，掩码仅对特定兼容模型有帮助，且场景保留能力弱；3. Track C中，参考图像 conditioning 导致所有模型性能下降，部分模型甚至重绘参考图本身，效果不如原图。专家评分验证了排名有效性（Kendall's tau = 0.68）。

Conclusion: 当前最强闭源编辑器已基本解决基于名称的材质渲染问题，但基于像素/图像的视觉 grounding 仍具挑战性，需进一步研究以提升真实世界中的材料替换泛化能力。

Abstract: Material replacement is a common interior-design operation: changing the material of a selected surface while preserving its geometry, surroundings, and illumination. Despite its commercial relevance, no public benchmark isolates this task, and evaluating it is challenging. Reference-based metrics penalize valid outputs in this inherently one-to-many setting, favor the style of the reference generator, and cannot fairly compare editors that receive different forms of guidance. We introduce MatReplace, a reference-free benchmark that evaluates edits along four verifiable dimensions: local material correctness, global lighting harmony, outside preservation, and inside structure. It defines three tracks that vary one conditioning signal at a time: (A) instruction only, (B) instruction plus region mask, and (C) material reference image instead of instruction. Our results reveal a clear divide between naming and visually grounding materials. In Track A, leading closed-source editors achieve exemplar-level material rendering and surpass the exemplar anchor under our primary aggregate. In Track B, masks help only mask-compatible models with weak scene preservation, with task-paired, single-seed effects ranging from +0.137 to -0.090 across aligned model families. In Track C, reference-image conditioning degrades every family under both aggregates, by -0.031 to -0.508; in the worst cases, models repaint the reference image itself and perform worse than returning the input unchanged. Thus, named-material rendering is largely solved by the strongest closed editors on this distribution, but grounding materials from pixels remains an open challenge. Expert ratings validate our ranking (Kendall's tau = 0.68) and align with our aggregates more closely than GT-referenced or CLIP-based baselines.

</details>


### [43] [TransPhy: Visual In-Context Learning for Physically Grounded Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24119)
*Siyi Xie,Xuanke Shi,Jinsheng Quan,Haoran Tang,Zukai Chen,Lei Yang,Quan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhysVICL-74基准和TransPhy框架，解决视觉上下文学习中物理 grounded 变换的难题，实现从外观级到物理规则级的图像编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉上下文学习（VICL）方法主要关注外观级关系迁移，缺乏对依赖材料属性、几何形状、物体交互和环境条件的物理接地变换的支持。

Method: 1. 构建PhysVICL-74基准：包含74条物理变换规则和5,240个源-目标图像对，评估新实例迁移和未见规则泛化能力。2. 提出TransPhy框架：将任务分解为物理规则诱导和过渡对齐渲染，通过预测目标和基于局部过渡线索的专家路由合成图像。

Result: TransPhy在物理规则遵循性、查询一致性和未见规则泛化方面优于现有的视觉上下文编辑方法。

Conclusion: TransPhy能有效推断演示变换并将其适应于特定场景上下文，同时保留无关内容，提升了物理接地VICL的性能。

Abstract: Visual demonstrations provide a natural interface for specifying image transformations that are difficult to describe exhaustively with text. However, existing visual in-context learning (VICL) methods primarily focus on appearance-level relation transfer and provide limited support for physically grounded transformations, whose outcomes depend on material properties, geometry, object interactions, and environmental conditions. Given a source--target exemplar pair and a query image, physically grounded VICL requires a model to infer the demonstrated transformation, adapt its effects to the query-specific scene context, and preserve rule-irrelevant content. We introduce PhysVICL-74, comprising 74 physically grounded transformation rules and 5,240 source--target image pairs that form nearly 75K training and evaluation contexts. Its benchmark split separately evaluates novel-instance transfer and unseen-rule generalization. We further propose TransPhy, a framework that decomposes physically grounded VICL into physical-rule induction and transition-aligned rendering. TransPhy first predicts the demonstrated rule and an explicit query-specific target-state description, and then synthesizes the target image through token-wise mixture-of-experts adaptation, with expert routing guided by localized transition cues. Experiments show that TransPhy improves physical-rule adherence, query consistency, and unseen-rule generalization over existing visual in-context editing methods.

</details>


### [44] [Graph-Supervised Hierarchical Clinical Alignment for Radiology Report Generation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24121)
*Yingshu Li,Yunyi Liu,Zhanyu Wang,Zailong Chen,Lingqiao Liu,Lei Wang,Luping Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Graph-Supervised Hierarchical Clinical Alignment方法，通过疾病中心对齐和全局临床语义对齐解决放射学报告生成中的粒度不匹配问题，利用临床知识图谱作为训练先验，在MIMIC-CXR等数据集上超越更大参数模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前放射学报告生成（RRG）主要依赖报告级监督，导致与由疾病支撑的发现组成的报告之间存在粒度不匹配，难以实现临床忠实性生成。

Method: 将图像-报告监督重构为层级临床对齐问题：1) 疾病中心对齐用于细粒度疾病特异性对应；2) 全局临床语义对齐确保报告级语义连贯性。使用临床知识图谱作为仅训练时的结构先验，结合实例条件判别匹配与疾病条件软正则化以处理重叠病理导致的假阴性。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR、IU-Xray和COV-CTR数据集上，该方法在常规指标和临床指标上均显著提升性能。特别是3B参数模型的性能超过了先前更大的7B/13B骨干网络系统。

Conclusion: 改进监督结构比单纯增加模型规模对提升放射学报告生成效果更为有效。

Abstract: Radiology report generation (RRG) has recently benefited from large language models, which substantially improve report fluency. However, clinically faithful generation remains challenging because current supervision is still imposed mostly at the report level. This creates a granularity mismatch: radiology reports are composed of disease-grounded findings, while existing methods are trained mainly with whole-report objectives. To address this problem, we propose Graph-Supervised Hierarchical Clinical Alignment, which reformulates image-report supervision as a hierarchical clinical alignment problem. Our method structures this alignment as a disease-conditioned process, where supervision is decomposed into two levels: Disease-Centric Alignment for fine-grained disease-specific correspondence, and Global Clinical Semantic Alignment for report-level semantic coherence. A clinical knowledge graph is used as a training-time-only structural prior that defines disease-specific supervision units and their clinical relationships, introducing no additional overhead at inference. Because standard contrastive alignment could produce false negatives when studies share overlapping pathologies, we combine instance-conditioned discriminative matching with disease-conditioned soft regularization, enabling fine-grained yet clinically consistent cross-modal representations. Experiments on MIMIC-CXR, IU-Xray, and COV-CTR show that our method consistently improves performance on both conventional and clinical metrics. Notably, our 3B model surpasses several prior systems with larger 7B/13B backbones, suggesting that improving supervision structure, rather than increasing model size, can be more effective for RRG.

</details>


### [45] [Syn2RealTrack: Bridging the Gap Between Synthetic and Real-World Datasets for Online Multi-View Multi-Target Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24130)
*Duong Nguyen-Ngoc Tran,Ngoc Doan-Minh Huynh,Cu Quoc Le,Hoang-Khang Nguyen,Long Hoang Pham,Huy-Hung Nguyen,Quoc Pham-Nam Ho,Trinh Le Ba Khanh,Chi Dai Tran,Duong Khac Vu,Son Hong Phan,Hyung-Min Jeon,Jae Wook Jeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Syn2RealTrack在线流水线，通过分解合成到真实的差距（相机标定、物体形状先验、已知物体普查假设）来解决仓库场景多相机3D感知问题，在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 1中取得52.0118%的HOTA成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多相机3D感知系统严重依赖合成数据训练并在真实环境中评估，导致显著的“合成到真实”（synthetic-to-real）差距，进而破坏地面定位和跨相机身份关联。传统方法通常试图用单一域适应模块吸收这一缺陷，作者认为该差距实际上在三个可分离的点进入流程：相机标定、物体形状先验和对物体普查已知的假设，每个点需要不同的局部补救措施。

Method: 提出了Syn2RealTrack在线流水线，包含四个关键改进：1) 仅从图像恢复镜头失真，无需初始标定；2) 使用基于可见性的加权部分描述符融合跨视图检测，对遮挡部分不予猜测；3) 根据标定信息以闭合形式测量人身高，而非复制合成先验；4) 将封闭世界基数先验与因果滤波器结合，消除先验制造的幻影框。该系统通过重新分配几何与外观之间的信任来进行适应，而无需重新训练特征提取器。

Result: 在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 1评估服务器上，该系统达到了3D高阶跟踪准确率（HOTA）为52.0118%。

Conclusion: 通过将合成到真实的差距分解为三个独立环节并分别处理，该方法无需重训练特征提取器即可有效适应真实环境，提升了多相机3D感知的性能。代码将在GitHub上开源。

Abstract: Multi-camera 3D perception systems for warehouse scenes are trained largely on synthetic data and evaluated on physically captured environments. The resulting synthetic-to-real gap, which corrupts ground-plane localization and cross-camera identity association, is usually treated as one deficiency for a single domain-adaptation module to absorb; we argue instead that it enters the pipeline at three separable points: the camera calibration, the object shape prior, and the assumption that the object census is known, each admitting a different local remedy. Our online pipeline, Syn2RealTrack, follows this decomposition: lens distortion is recovered from images alone under a calibration that provides none, detections are fused across views by a visibility-weighted part-based descriptor that abstains on occluded parts rather than guessing, person height is measured in closed form from calibration instead of copied from a synthetic prior, and a closed-world cardinality prior is paired with a causal filter that removes the phantom boxes the prior manufactures. The system therefore adapts by reallocating trust between geometry and appearance without retraining a feature extractor. On the AI City Challenge 2026 Track~1 evaluation server it reaches a 3D Higher Order Tracking Accuracy (HOTA) of 52.0118%. The code will be released at https://github.com/SKKUAutoLab/aic26_mc3dp

</details>


### [46] [EgoErrorVQA: Assess Egocentric Comprehension Capabilities through Procedural Errors for Ego-Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24134)
*Junlong Li,Junxi Li,Jianjun Gao,Chen Cai,Lap-Pui Chau,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EgoErrorVQA任务以评估视觉智能体在自我中心视角下的程序性理解能力，特别是检测程序错误的能力。开发基于A2A协议的评估器，并引入Ego-ADR框架提升模型对程序错误的理解，实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试忽视了从自我中心视觉视角评估视觉智能体和视觉语言模型（VLMs）的程序性理解能力，尤其是检测日常活动中关键的程序错误能力。

Method: 1. 提出EgoErrorVQA任务，显式建模程序错误；2. 基于Agent2Agent (A2A)协议开发用户友好的评估器代理，通过VQA交互进行标准化评估；3. 提出Ego-ADR（自适应解耦推理）框架，将复杂的程序推理解耦以增强对程序错误的理解。

Result: 现有模型在处理程序错误和特定错误类型上存在持续弱点；提出的Ego-ADR框架在多种指标上取得一致的性能提升，并在可比设置下达到最先进（SOTA）水平。

Conclusion: EgoErrorVQA填补了自我中心程序性理解评估的空白，Ego-ADR有效提升了模型在该任务上的表现，为日常辅助场景下的视觉智能体提供了更可靠的评估与优化方向。

Abstract: The majority of our everyday activities are procedural and consist of sequences of interdependent steps. However, existing benchmarks for Visual Agents and Visual Language Models (VLMs) overlook the evaluation of their procedural comprehension ability from an egocentric visual perspective, particularly for detecting procedural errors, a critical capability for everyday assistance. To bridge this gap, the EgoErrorVQA task is firstly proposed for egocentric procedural comprehension with explicit procedural errors modeling. Besides, we develop a user-friendly evaluator agent based on the Agent2Agent (A2A) protocol, enabling rigorous and standardized evaluation of visual agents through VQA-based interaction. A range of models are evaluated using both open-ended and multiple-choice questions, revealing persistent weaknesses in handling procedural errors and error types. Moreover, we introduce Ego-ADR, an Adaptive Decoupled Reasoning framework that decouples complex procedural reasoning to enhance models' understanding of procedural errors. It achieves consistent performance gains over the selected baselines and attains state-of-the-art results on several metrics under comparable settings. Code: https://github.com/z1oong/EgoErrorVQA

</details>


### [47] [Rubrics as Visual-Repair Context for Self-Evolving UI-to-Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24138)
*Tianyi Xiong,Zhengyuan Yang,Xiaofei Wang,Chung-Ching Lin,Ruichun Ma,Kevin Lin,Zhendong Wang,Linjie Li,Chenxi Liu,Ruibo Chen,Ramani Duraiswami,Heng Huang,Lijuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RubSE框架，通过结构化视觉反馈（rubrics）解决大视觉语言模型在UI转代码生成中测试时自我进化不稳定的问题，显著提升修复稳定性和性能上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大视觉语言模型在UI-to-code生成中，测试时的自我进化过程不稳定，主要障碍是“视觉修复耦合”现象：局部代码编辑可能因布局、样式和组件依赖关系产生连锁反应，修正一处视觉错误的同时恶化其他区域。

Method: 提出RubSE（Rubric-guided Self-Evolution），利用rubrics将视觉反馈表示为结构化的视觉修复上下文。在每轮优化中，生成类型化候选rubrics，选择优先级最高的修复目标，并存储历史rubrics以引导每次修订聚焦于特定范围的视觉修复，避免重复或过度宽泛的更改。

Result: 在六个VLMs和三个UI-to-code基准上的评估显示，RubSE在最终轮和最佳轮设置下显著优于朴素自我进化方法，实现了更稳定的优化轨迹和更高的性能上限；分析表明其能通过改善从严重视觉退化中的恢复来缓解轨迹崩溃，且更强的rubric生成器能有效指导较弱的代码改进器。

Conclusion: RubSE通过结构化视觉反馈机制有效解决了视觉修复耦合导致的自我进化不稳定问题，提升了UI-to-code生成的鲁棒性和最终质量，证明了结构化引导在模型迭代优化中的有效性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have shown strong progress in UI-to-code generation, yet their test-time self-evolution remains unstable. We first identify a fundamental obstacle, termed visual repair coupling: a local code edit may propagate through layout, style, and component dependencies, correcting one visual mismatch while degrading regions that were previously faithful. To address this issue, we present RubSE, a Rubric-guided Self-Evolution framework that uses rubrics to represent visual feedback as a structured visual-repair context. At each refinement round, RubSE generates typed candidate rubrics, selects one prioritized repair target, and stores previously selected rubrics as history, thereby steering each revision toward a well-scoped visual repair while discouraging repeated or over-broad changes. Evaluations across six VLMs and three UI-to-code benchmarks demonstrate that RubSE substantially outperforms naïve self-evolution in final-round and best-round settings, achieving more stable refinement trajectories and a higher trajectory-level performance ceiling. Further analysis shows that RubSE mitigates trajectory collapse by improving recovery from severe visual regressions, and that stronger rubric generators can transfer effective visual-repair guidance to weaker code improvers.

</details>


### [48] [What Does Prompt Learning Change? -A Natural-Language Concept Analysis of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24142)
*Ryo Kamiya,Hiroshi Kera,Kazuhiko Kawamoto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PromptSpLiCE，一种后处理方法，将视觉语言模型（如CLIP）学习到的连续提示向量解释为自然语言词典中概念的稀疏组合，以解决可解释性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的提示学习方法（如优化连续提示向量）虽然有效，但生成的提示难以用自然语言解释，缺乏直观性。

Method: 使用固定的自然语言词典，将每个类别条件文本嵌入表示为概念的稀疏组合；通过对比提示学习前后的概念分布变化进行分析；在CoOp方法上评估，并推导局部梯度表达式以提供几何直觉。

Result: 在11个图像分类数据集上评估发现：学习后概念分布发生显著变化（初始前10概念中平均仅1.6个保留在前10）；概念分布的变化幅度与准确率提升呈正相关；推导出的局部梯度表达式解释了为何与当前提示不同的图像对齐概念方向可能具有更高的损失敏感度。

Conclusion: PromptSpLiCE成功实现了提示向量的自然语言可解释性分析，揭示了提示学习中概念选择的动态机制，并建立了概念演变与模型性能提升之间的联系。

Abstract: Prompt learning adapts vision-language models such as CLIP by optimizing continuous prompt vectors, but the learned prompts are difficult to interpret in natural language. We present PromptSpLiCE, a post-hoc method that expresses each class-conditioned text embedding as a sparse combination of concepts from a fixed natural-language dictionary. Using the same dictionary before and after prompt learning allows us to compare changes in their concept profiles. We evaluate PromptSpLiCE on CoOp, a representative prompt-learning method, across 11 image-classification datasets. The concept profiles change substantially: on average, only 1.6 of the initial top-10 concepts remain in the top 10 after learning. Across datasets, profile change is positively associated with accuracy gain. We also derive a local gradient expression that provides geometric intuition for why image-aligned concept directions distinct from the current prompt can have greater loss sensitivity.

</details>


### [49] [Rethinking Pre-Training and Augmentation for Zero-Shot Cross-City Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24154)
*Long Hoang Pham,Quoc Pham-Nam Ho,Huy-Hung Nguyen,Duong Nguyen-Ngoc Tran,Ngoc Doan-Minh Huynh,Cu Quoc Le,Hoang-Khang Nguyen,Hyung-Min Jeon,Chi Dai Tran,Son Hong Phan,Duong Khac Vu,Trinh Le Ba Khanh,Jae Wook Jeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种模块化训练流水线，通过多数据集预训练和灰度世界增强解决交通监控中的地理域偏移问题，在AI City Challenge Track 6中获得冠军。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实部署中，由于地理域偏移（在不同城市模型性能下降），传统域适应方法依赖超参数敏感架构或直接分析目标数据，这在需要完全盲训的隐私敏感环境中不可行。

Method: 提出基于两个核心正交支柱的模块化训练流水线：(1) 多数据集预训练策略，包含类别无关物体性蒸馏，以解耦车辆几何结构与语义分类；(2) 域稳健增强流，引入新型Grayworld变换，迫使全局注意力头剥离易变的色彩捷径，转而依赖鲁棒的形状先验。使用RF-DETR检测器进行评估。

Result: 该方法在有限GPU内存（16GB）下跨越了跨城市分布差距。优化变体RF-DETR-HR和RF-DETR-Grayworld相比基线显著提升24.29 mAP，以47.53 mAP的成绩在AI City Challenge Track 6排行榜上获得第一名。

Conclusion: 通过结合类别无关物体性蒸馏和Grayworld增强，有效解决了隐私约束下的跨域交通检测问题，实现了高性能且资源高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world deployment of traffic surveillance systems is bottlenecked by geographic domain shift, in which models trained in one city underperform when applied to an unseen target city. Conventional domain adaptation relies on hyperparameter-sensitive architectures or direct profiling of target data. Both are fundamentally precluded in privacy-conscious ecosystems that require completely blind training and evaluation loops. In this setting, we explore the effects of pre-training and augmentation in addressing the domain shift problem. Specifically, we propose a new modular training pipeline for object detection structured around two core orthogonal pillars: (1) a multi-dataset pre-training strategy featuring a class-agnostic objectness distillation to decouple structural vehicle geometry from semantic taxonomies, and (2) a domain-resilient augmentation stream featuring a novel Grayworld transformation that forces global attention heads to strip volatile chromatic shortcuts in favor of robust shape priors. When evaluated with the real-time transformer-based detector RF-DETR, our framework bridges cross-city distribution gaps while using limited GPU memory (16GB). Our optimized variants, RF-DETR-HR and RF-DETR-Grayworld, deliver a substantial empirical gain of +24.29 over the baseline, achieving 1st place (47.53 mAP) on the AI City Challenge Track 6 leaderboard. Code and data are available at: \href{https://github.com/SKKUAutoLab/aic26_cross_city}{SKKUAutoLab/aic26\_cross\_city}.

</details>


### [50] [ViSculpt: Visual-Centric Agentic Geometry Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24169)
*Bo Pang,Jiaqi Pan,Xiaocheng Zhang,Jiacheng Xu,Guoping Wang,Peng-Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的视觉中心多智能体系统，通过模拟人类艺术家在Blender中的交互流程，直接对现有3D网格进行自然语言驱动的局部编辑，而非生成脚本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统3D几何编辑劳动密集且依赖复杂软件操作的问题；弥补大型语言模型（LLMs）在基于感知驱动的任意现有网格编辑中，脚本生成方式缺乏视觉 grounding 且难以保留未修改区域的问题。

Method: 构建一个训练-free的多智能体系统，直接在Blender GUI中运行。多模态LLM智能体观察视口、推理当前网格状态，并通过模拟用户交互执行本地化编辑，模仿人类艺术家的迭代工作流程。

Result: 在定制基准测试中，该系统能够遵循自然语言指令，执行代表性的局部网格编辑，并保持输入资产的整体身份不变。

Conclusion: 确立了语言驱动3D编辑的一种互补范式：在原生3D编辑工作流中对现有网格进行直接的就地修改。该工作是为专业图形软件中视觉中心化的代理几何编辑迈出的探索性一步。

Abstract: 3D geometry editing is a critical yet labor-intensive part of the graphics pipeline, requiring artists to translate creative intent into precise operations in complex professional software. Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for script-based 3D creation, but script generation is less suited to perception-driven editing of arbitrary existing meshes, where execution must remain visually grounded and untouched regions should be preserved. We present a \emph{visual-centric}, training-free multi-agent system that edits existing 3D meshes directly in Blender by emulating the iterative workflow of human artists. Rather than generating scripts or regenerating geometry, our system operates through the Blender GUI: multimodal LLM agents observe the viewport, reason about the current mesh state, and execute localized edits through simulated user interactions. Experiments on a curated benchmark provide initial evidence that this agentic approach can follow natural language instructions, perform representative localized mesh edits, and preserve the overall identity of the input asset. Our results highlight a complementary regime for language-driven 3D editing: direct in-place modification of existing meshes within the native 3D editing workflow. We view this work as an exploratory step toward visual-centric agentic geometry editing in professional graphics software.

</details>


### [51] [SandwichQuant: Which Parameters Matter Before and After Quantization?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24173)
*Peng Xia,Junbiao Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SandwichQuant框架，通过分析参数子空间发现归一化仿射参数是高效校正方向，采用量化前后两阶段校正策略，在视觉和大语言模型的低比特量化中显著提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化校正方法多优化权重、量化参数或重构目标，但缺乏对负责有效校正的底层参数子空间的清晰认知。

Method: 从参数子空间视角研究量化校正，将可训练参数分解为骨干权重、归一化仿射参数和量化参数；提出SandwichQuant，这是一种基于归一化仿射校正的两阶段框架，包含量化前的适应阶段（提升鲁棒性）和量化后的补偿阶段（修复残差误差）。

Result: 实验显示，在视觉模型和大语言模型的多种低比特量化设置下，该方法均带来一致的性能提升，验证了子空间对齐校正的有效性。

Conclusion: 归一化仿射子空间提供了高效的校正方向，SandwichQuant通过两阶段策略有效提升了量化模型的性能。

Abstract: Quantization correction methods usually optimize weights, quantization parameters, or reconstruction objectives, while the underlying parameter subspaces responsible for effective correction remain unclear. In this work, we study quantization correction from a parameter subspace perspective and reveal that correction capability is highly non-uniform across parameter groups. By decomposing trainable parameters into backbone weights, normalization-affine parameters, and quantization parameters, we show that the low-dimensional normalization-affine subspace provides a highly efficient correction direction under matched budgets. Based on this finding, we propose SandwichQuant, a two-stage normalization-affine correction framework that performs adaptation before and after quantization. The pre-stage improves quantization robustness, while the post-stage compensates residual errors after the quantized graph is fixed. Extensive experiments on vision models and large language models demonstrate consistent improvements under various low-bit quantization settings, validating the effectiveness of subspace-aligned correction.

</details>


### [52] [Amortized Set Prediction for Inverse IFS Reconstruction from Density Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24175)
*Yutaka Yamaguti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于学习估计器的迭代函数系统（IFS）参数逆向求解方法，通过从访问频率密度图直接预测仿射映射集，替代传统的逐图像优化，显著提升了重建速度与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决IFS中从图像反演参数这一计算困难且通常依赖逐图像优化的逆问题，旨在通过单次前向传播的 learned estimator 来摊销逆问题的计算成本。

Method: 1. 使用学习估计器直接从访问频率密度图预测仿射映射集；2. 采用匈牙利匹配处理无序映射集，利用真实参数作为训练代理以稳定训练；3. 利用已知正向模型生成精确的合成训练对，并支持测试时的仅图像细化。

Result: 1. 在分布内测试中，摊销初始化加少量细化步骤在质量-速度权衡上优于同等预算的随机初始化逐图像优化；2. 在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST真实图像上，平均密度指标优于已发布的逐图像优化器，且速度提升12至2600倍；3. 长步数优化显示摊销初始化能更频繁地达到高质量重建。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅大幅加速了IFS参数估计过程，还在重建质量上表现出优势，证明了摊销逆问题求解在效率与效果上的双重收益。

Abstract: Iterated Function Systems (IFS) generate self-similar fractals from a few contractive affine maps. The forward map from parameters to images is computationally inexpensive and well understood, whereas the inverse problem of estimating maps from an image is difficult and is typically handled by per-image optimization. We replace this loop with a single forward pass of a learned estimator that predicts the affine-map set directly from a visit-frequency density map, thereby amortizing the inverse problem. The design follows two constraints. First, density maps do not uniquely identify IFS parameters, so evaluation is based on reconstruction rather than parameter recovery; unordered map sets are handled by Hungarian matching, and ground-truth parameters provide a stable training surrogate. Second, the fully known forward model lets us generate exact synthetic training pairs and also supports image-only test-time refinement. On in-distribution tests, amortized initialization plus a few refinement steps lies on a better quality--speed frontier than equal-budget random-initialized per-image optimization, and a 30-step refinement (about $0.56$ s per sample) remains better than a doubled-budget baseline. Extending optimization to 1000 steps shows that the benefit is not only speed: amortized initialization reaches high-quality reconstructions more frequently than random starts. On real images (MNIST and Fashion-MNIST), it improves density metrics on average over a published per-image optimizer while being roughly 12 to 2600 times faster.

</details>


### [53] [NeoWorld-Pro: Programming Interactive Scenes from Monocular Images for Embodied Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24212)
*Yumeng He,Yichen Song,Xiaotian Yang,Weijia Zhang,Zanwei Zhou,Junru Gong,Xiaokang Yang,Yunbo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NeoWorld-Pro 利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）将单目 RGB 图像转化为可执行的程序，并通过物理引擎循环验证与迭代优化，生成具备物理合理性和交互能力的 3D 仿真场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有图像到 URDF 方法缺乏物理基础和环境级交互能力的问题，为具身智能提供高保真、可交互的仿真资产。

Method: 提出 NeoWorld-Pro 框架：1) 利用 MLLMs 的零样本推理和代码合成能力，将单目图像转换为指定物体几何、关节结构和物理属性的可执行程序；2) 引入“物理在环”（physics-in-the-loop）机制，在物理引擎中执行并验证程序，迭代修正以确保证合物体组合、交互及空间关系的物理合理性。

Result: 实验表明，NeoWorld-Pro 优于开环方法及先前单目重建方法，能够支持稳定堆叠和细粒度操作等复杂下游任务。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了从视觉观测到交互式 3D 仿真环境的转化，提升了具身智能仿真的质量和实用性。

Abstract: The advancement of Embodied AI necessitates high-quality simulation assets that faithfully mirror the real world. However, transforming raw visual observations into simulation-ready scenes remains challenging due to the lack of physical grounding and scene-level interactivity in current image-to-URDF methods. We propose NeoWorld-Pro, a framework that reformulates monocular scene reconstruction as procedural programming for interactive 3D environments. Leveraging the zero-shot reasoning and code synthesis capabilities of MLLMs, NeoWorld-Pro converts a single RGB image into executable programs specifying object geometry, articulation, and physical properties. A physics-in-the-loop mechanism then iteratively refines the generated programs by validating their execution in a physics engine, enforcing physically plausible articulations, valid object compositions and interactions, and accurate spatial relationships. Experiments show that NeoWorld-Pro outperforms open-loop and prior monocular reconstruction methods, while enabling complex downstream tasks such as stable stacking and fine-grained manipulation.

</details>


### [54] [Beauty is in the ELBO of the Beholder: A Variational Account of Processing Fluency in Face Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24219)
*Francisco M. López,Jochen Triesch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究通过变分自编码器（VAE）实证检验了“面部吸引力取决于感知流畅度”的假设。研究发现，人类对面部吸引力的评分与VAE在率失真空间中的证据下界（ELBO）方向高度一致，且吸引力方向具有跨初始化数据的强迁移性，证明吸引力面孔在形状和潜在空间中更具典型性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证面部吸引力是否与统计规律（如对称性和平均性）相关，进而测试“美取决于面部被感知的难易程度（即处理流畅度）”这一假设。

Method: 在无吸引力监督的情况下，使用四个面部数据集训练变分自编码器（VAE），并在芝加哥面部数据库（Chicago Face Database）的597张面孔上评估其表征。重点分析VAE证据下界（ELBO）在率失真空间中的方向与人类吸引力评分的一致性，以及独立学习的潜在空间中吸引力方向的稳定性。

Result: 1.  Across models, human attractiveness ratings closely aligns with the direction defined by the VAE evidence lower bound (ELBO) in rate-distortion space.
2.  Independently learned latent spaces contain an attractiveness direction that transfers strongly across random initializations and training data.
3.  Attractive faces are more prototypical in both shape and latent space.

Conclusion: 研究结果将经典美学理论与学习到的生成模型联系起来，为审美愉悦的处理流畅度理论提供了基于变分解释的经验支持，表明面部吸引力可以通过生成模型的统计特性来量化。

Abstract: Facial attractiveness has been linked to statistical regularities such as symmetry and averageness, suggesting that beauty may depend on the ease with which a face is perceived. We empirically test this hypothesis by training variational autoencoders on four face datasets without attractiveness supervision and evaluating their representations on the 597 faces from the Chicago Face Database. Across models, human attractiveness ratings closely aligns with the direction defined by the VAE evidence lower bound (ELBO) in rate-distortion space. Independently learned latent spaces contain an attractiveness direction that transfers strongly across random initializations and training data. We also find that attractive faces are more prototypical in both shape and latent space. Our results connect classic accounts of aesthetics with learned generative models and provide empirical support for a variational interpretation of the processing fluency theory of aesthetic pleasure.

</details>


### [55] [Event-Based Motion Estimation via Oriented Distance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24223)
*Lei Sun,Yuqin Ma,Weilun Li,Haoran Liang,Runyi Yang,Kaiwei Wang,Danda Pani Paudel,Luc Van Gool*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ODF运动估计方法，通过预计算场的单次平均替代迭代优化，实现低延迟亚像素精度，并成功应用于实时去模糊和低功耗眼动追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于事件的运动估计方法依赖迭代优化或重复假设比较，抵消了传感器低延迟优势；需解决高速运动下的高时间分辨率与鲁棒性需求。

Method: 提出定向距离场运动估计（ODF Motion Estimation），核心为对预计算的事件距离向量场进行单次平均，结合自适应事件计数选择策略和无参数轨迹滤波。

Result: 在公开及自收集数据集上达到最低延迟下的亚像素精度；去模糊任务以少于1M参数实现竞争级PSNR/SSIM；眼动追踪任务维持数十秒稳定跟踪并降低近眼模块功耗。

Conclusion: ODF运动估计不仅提升了速度精度平衡，且其通用性经过去模糊和眼动追踪两项下游应用验证，证明了其在实时低功耗视觉系统中的有效性。

Abstract: Event-based motion estimation is central to tasks that demand high temporal resolution and robustness to fast motion. Existing methods typically rely on iterative optimization or repeated hypothesis comparison, offsetting the sensor's low-latency advantage. We propose Oriented Distance Field Motion Estimation (ODF Motion Estimation), which replaces this optimization with a single averaging step over a precomputed field of event distance vectors, combined with an adaptive event-count selection strategy and a parameter-free trail filter. On public and self-collected datasets, ODF motion estimation reaches sub-pixel accuracy at the lowest latency among compared methods. We validate its generality on two downstream applications rather than treating them as separate contributions. First, the estimated trajectory is converted into a blur kernel and paired with a compact iterative-unfolding network, trained on simulated motion-estimation noise, for real-time non-blind image deblurring, attaining competitive or superior PSNR/SSIM with under 1M parameters. Second, the same precomputed field is repurposed for directional event filtering in a low-power asynchronous pupil and glint tracker, sustaining stable tracking for tens of seconds while lowering a near-eye module's power draw.

</details>


### [56] [Example-based Robust Abnormality Detection with Minimal Annotations using Exemplar Med-DETR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24281)
*Sheethal Bhat,Bogdan Georgescu,Awais Mansoor,Mathias Zinnen,Pranjal Sahu,Florin C. Ghesu,Sasa Grbic,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于EM-DETR的可扩展少样本检测框架，用于在极少标注数据下高效检测胸部X光片异常，实现了近SOTA性能且仅需不到10%的标注数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学对象检测面临标注成本高、缺乏高质量接地文本数据的挑战；现有的视觉语言方法和少样本学习在泛化到新病灶或需要重新训练方面存在局限，未能充分利用医学图像中的上下文和非成像信息。

Method: 扩展了EM-DETR框架，引入了一种面向CXR图像的少样本检测方法。该架构结合了基于示例的特征生成和领域感知对比优化，以实现对新疾病发现的有效适应。

Result: 该方法在使用少于10%标注数据的情况下，达到了接近最先进（SOTA）的检测性能，并在专有和公共CXR数据集上验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了其在减少标注依赖方面的潜力，适合在实际临床部署中实现高效、低标注成本的异常检测。

Abstract: Reducing annotation requirements remains a key challenge in developing robust medical object detectors. To address this, Vision-Language (VL) object detection methods leverage grounding text information to enable powerful zero-shot and few-shot object detectors in the natural image domain [1, 2, 3, 4]. However, transferring these methods to the medical domain is challenging due to the absence of comparable quality and quantity of the grounding data. Regardless, significant contextual and non-imaging information exists in medical images that remains underutilized. Few-shot learning (FSL) techniques partially address this limitation but struggle to general ize to unseen medical findings and require extensive retraining when new findings are introduced [5, 6]. To overcome these challenges, we extend our prior EM-DETR framework [7] and introduce a scalable FS detection approach designed for efficient abnormality detection in Chest X-Ray (CXR) images under minimal supervision. The proposed architecture incorporates exemplar-based feature generation and domain-aware contrastive optimization, enabling effective adaptation to novel disease findings without exhaustive retraining. Our method achieves near state-of-the-art (SOTA) detection performance using less than 10% of the annotated data, demonstrating its potential for practical, annotation-efficient clinical deployment across both proprietary and public CXR datasets.

</details>


### [57] [CARE: Camera-Residual Reserves for First Sightings in Adaptive LiDAR Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24282)
*Jiachen Gong,Yun Li,Ehsan Javanmardi,Wencan Mao,Manabu Tsukada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CARE方法，通过预留部分激光雷达扫描预算给相机检测到但轨迹预测未覆盖的区域，解决自适应LiDAR扫描中未见物体检测滞后、随机采样无意识及相机引导资源浪费的问题，提升首次发现召回率并保证安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应LiDAR扫描策略面临三大挑战：1）基于历史的依赖导致未见物体检测滞后或遗漏；2）区域外随机/均匀采样缺乏对新物体出现位置的感知；3）相机引导策略在拥挤场景或预算有限时因重复扫描已覆盖物体而降低召回率和探测范围。

Method: 提出无需训练的分配规则CARE（CAmera-REsidual reserve）：将固定光线预算的一部分预留用于当前相机检测到但轨迹预测无法解释的方向；剩余部分遵循基础历史策略；未使用的预留预算回归随机底层分布。此外，引入受安全约束的遗忘模块，根据速度相关的安全距离释放远离或静止轨迹的预算。

Result: 1）构建了nuScenes数据集上无泄漏的光线预算评估基准；2）在10%、20%、35%预算下，CARE相比历史策略分别将首次发现召回率提升5.2、5.2和4.3个百分点；3）安全遗忘模块防止了在紧预算下近场召回率的显著下降；闭环仿真显示能更早检测遮挡行人并更可靠刹车。

Conclusion: CARE有效平衡了首次发现召回率与整体性能，通过结合相机线索和历史轨迹优化预算分配，并在真实车辆端到端管道及闭环仿真中验证了其安全性和有效性，优于纯历史驱动扫描策略。

Abstract: Adaptive LiDAR scanning concentrates a limited sensing budget on regions of interest predicted from past object tracks, lowering data volume in autonomous driving while maintaining detection accuracy. However, existing scanning policies face three challenges. First, history-driven approaches depend on past tracks, so unseen objects are detected late or missed. Second, random or uniform sampling outside the predicted regions has no awareness of where new objects appear. Third, camera-guided alternatives spend budget on all camera detections, resampling objects already covered, costing recall in crowded scenes and range when budgets are scarce. This paper introduces the CAmera-REsidual reserve (CARE), a training-free allocation rule that reserves part of a fixed ray budget for the directions of current camera detections that the track forecasts cannot explain; the rest follows the base history policy, and unused reserve returns to a random floor. The paper makes three contributions. First, a leakage-free ray-budget evaluation on nuScenes (150 scenes, 4,148 events) measuring the first-sighting loss of history-driven scanning, with a strict-causal variant using the preceding keyframe. Second, CARE raises first-sighting recall by 5.2, 5.2, and 4.3 points at 10%, 20%, and 35% budgets over the history policy, with paired intervals excluding zero; the camera cue drives this gain, and the first-sighting versus overall trade-off is a budget-dependent Pareto choice. Third, a safety-bounded forgetting module that releases budget from receding or static tracks beyond a speed-dependent guard distance; at tight budgets, forgetting without the guard significantly harms near-field recall, so the guard is what keeps it safe. The pipeline runs end to end on a real vehicle and, in closed-loop simulation, detects an occluded pedestrian earlier and brakes more reliably than history-driven scanning.

</details>


### [58] [Keep-or-Drop? Adaptive Tokenizer for Compact Video Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24293)
*Yeonkyeong Lee,Hyunsung Go,Jongmin Kim,Sewoong Lim,Donghoon Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出KATok，一种基于Transformer的自适应视频Tokenizer，通过动态丢弃非信息性Token实现高效压缩，解决传统VAE固定压缩比无法适应视频时空复杂性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变分自编码器（VAE）在视频生成中采用固定压缩率，无法适应视频数据多变的时空内容复杂度，导致效率与质量难以兼顾。

Method: 1. 设计KATok：引入联合学习的自适应Token选择器，根据Token的内容丰富度计算保留或丢弃概率，实现数据依赖的动态压缩；2. 提出级联和联合生成两种位置预测策略，以缓解因Token丢弃导致的时空结构错位问题。

Result: 模型在先进的压缩比下实现了卓越的重构和生成质量；定量和定性分析表明，性能提升主要源于减少时空冗余及移除无信息Token。

Conclusion: KATok通过自适应Token化有效平衡了视频生成的计算效率与视觉保真度，为高保真视频合成提供了更优的潜在空间表示方案。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models have emerged as a dominant framework for high-fidelity image and video synthesis, operating in compact latent spaces with variational autoencoders (VAEs) to enhance computational efficiency without compromising visual quality. However, conventional VAEs are suboptimal for video data as they employ fixed compression ratios that cannot adapt to the varying complexity of spatio-temporal content. We present KATok (Keep-or-Drop? Adaptive Tokenizer for Compact Video Representation), a transformer-based VAE that incorporates an adaptive token selector which is jointly learned with latent tokens. By evaluating each token's content-richness as keep-or-drop probability, the token selector effectively discards uninformative tokens, naturally allowing data-dependent compression. Applying adaptive tokenization to diffusion models may cause spatial misalignment, as token dropping can disturb the original spatio-temporal structure. To alleviate this issue, we propose two position-prediction strategies: cascaded and joint generation, to ensure spatial consistency. We empirically show that our model achieves strong reconstruction and generation quality at a state-of-the-art compression ratio. Further analysis on video data reveals that this improvement is primarily achieved by reducing spatio-temporal redundancy and removing uninformative tokens, as supported by both quantitative and qualitative results.

</details>


### [59] [SeMoCo: A Semantic-First Motion Codec for Motion Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24334)
*Tianlv Huang,Hetian Guo,Ziyi Cai,Song Wang,Yanping Zhang,Zipei Fan,Xuan Song,Guangming Wu,Xin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SeMoCo语义优先运动编解码器和双轴生成器，解决现有运动tokenizer未按语义角色分配容量的问题，并在大规模数据集Ω-MotionVerse上验证了其重建精度和文本到运动生成的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散运动表示虽推动了自回归文本到运动生成，但大多数运动tokenizer仅针对重建进行优化，未根据语义角色明确分配容量，导致动作级含义和细粒度运动细节被迫通过同一重建驱动层级编码。

Method: 引入SeMoCo（语义优先运动编解码器）及用于语言条件运动生成的双轴运动生成器。每个运动令牌包含一个语义令牌和一个运动学残差序列。生成器对时间上的语义进程建模，并自回归地细化残差条目。同时构建了基于SOMA表示的大规模多源人体运动数据集Ω-MotionVerse。

Result: 在对比实验中，SeMoCo实现了最佳的 reconstruction accuracy（重建准确率）；在文本到运动任务中，其运动令牌展现了强大的下游生成效果。

Conclusion: SeMoCo通过分离语义和运动学信息，有效提升了运动重建精度和文本到运动生成的性能。

Abstract: Discrete motion representations have substantially advanced autoregressive text-to-motion generation. However, most motion tokenizers are optimized for reconstruction and do not explicitly allocate capacity according to semantic role. Action-level meaning and fine-grained kinematic detail must therefore be encoded through the same reconstruction-driven hierarchy. We introduce SeMoCo, a semantic-first motion codec, together with a dual-axis motion generator for language-conditioned motion generation. Each motion token contains one semantic token and a residual sequence of kinematic tokens. The generator models semantic progression across time and autoregressively refines the residual entries. We also construct $Ω$-MotionVerse, a large-scale, multi-source human-motion dataset unified under the SOMA representation. Across the reported comparisons, SeMoCo achieves the best reconstruction accuracy among the compared codecs, while strong text-to-motion results demonstrate the effectiveness of its motion tokens for downstream generation.

</details>


### [60] [Mind the Student: Behavioral and Contextual Cues for Automated Engagement Prediction in Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24340)
*Alperen Kantarci,Visvanathan Ramesh,Gemma Roig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种多模态框架，通过整合视频、音频及结构化行为信号，并利用不确定性感知技术，解决在线辅导视频中因个体差异和标注主观性导致的学生参与度预测难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生参与度是多维结构（行为、情感、认知），且CASED数据集存在高个体差异性和标注主观性，导致现有方法在测试集上接近随机猜测水平，难以实现可靠预测。

Method: 构建多模态框架：1) 集成预训练编码器提取的时空特征与结构化行为模态（头部姿态、视线、面部动作单元等）；2) 使用Perceiver IO潜在瓶颈进行模态融合；3) 将学生和教师人格建模为可学习嵌入的变分后验以实现部分池化；4) 采用证据回归和谱归一化高斯过程分类头进行不确定性感知预测。

Result: 在CASED挑战基准测试中，尽管整体处于高模糊度 regime，该框架仍取得具有竞争力的性能，并唯一提供了校准良好的不确定性指标。

Conclusion: 可靠的风险分析是将在现实教育工具中部署参与度模型的必要前提条件，不确定性量化对于提升模型鲁棒性和实用性至关重要。

Abstract: The prediction of student engagement from the online tutoring videos is difficult because engagement is a multidimensional construct comprising distinct behavioral, emotional, and cognitive states. A reliable prediction requires bringing together different types of behavioral signals as well as expressive cues. Through our analysis of the CASED dataset, it is clear that engagement prediction gets even harder due to the high inter-person variability as well as the subjectivity of the engagement annotation. To tackle these challenges, we develop a multimodal framework that integrates the implicit spatiotemporal features extracted from pretrained video, audio, and image encoders along with structured behavioral modalities like head pose, gaze, facial action units, emotion, and wavelet-based audio features. We integrate these modalities via a Perceiver IO latent bottleneck. Moreover, student and instructor personalities are modeled as variational posteriors over learnable embeddings to enable partial pooling across participants. We employ evidential regression and spectral-normalized Gaussian process classification heads for uncertainty-aware prediction to further improve robustness and calibration. Benchmark on the CASED challenge test set shows that all participating methods converge near random-chance performance, revealing the difficulty of the dataset. In this highly ambiguous regime, our framework achieves competitive performance while uniquely offering well-calibrated uncertainty metrics, demonstrating that reliable risk-quantification is an essential prerequisite for deploying engagement models in real-world educational tools.

</details>


### [61] [Metadata-Aware Adaptation of a Generative Foundation Model for Conditional CMR Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24342)
*Marc Rodríguez,Grzegorz Skorupko,Nay Aung,Steffen E Petersen,Karim Lekadir,Polyxeni Gkontra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究利用预训练的潜在扩散模型，通过文本提示编码结构化临床元数据和切片位置，实现心脏磁共振（CMR）图像合成。为解决数据稀缺和临床属性不平衡问题，集成了无元数据分类器自由引导（CFG）、对比批处理和逆频率采样策略。在UK Biobank的59,058张短轴CMR图像上评估，该方法FID得分为37.47，较基线提升显著，证明了生成式基础模型在具有临床意义的CMR合成中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决医学影像中数据稀缺及重要临床表型代表性不足的问题；现有方法难以生成忠实反映有意义患者特征的图像。

Method: 使用预训练的潜在扩散模型进行元数据条件化的CMR合成。将结构化临床元数据和切片位置作为文本提示输入。引入三种策略以增强元数据依从性和处理临床属性不平衡：1) 无元数据分类器自由引导 (Metadata-Free CFG)；2) 对比批处理 (Contrastive Batching)；3) 逆频率采样 (Inverse-Frequency Sampling)。在UK Biobank数据集上进行微调。

Result: 结合所有策略的方法实现了37.47的Fréchet Inception Distance (FID)，比未采用这些策略的模型提升了57.04%，比需要心脏几何信息的先前基线提升了28.68%。分布性增益主要由无元数据CFG驱动，但伴随配对相似度的适度降低。子组分析显示人口统计学和采集相关元数据的对齐改善，疾病特异性条件化最具挑战性。

Conclusion: 生成式基础模型具有用于具有临床意义CMR合成的潜力，但需要更有效的元数据感知条件化策略。代码已开源。

Abstract: Synthetic image generation is a promising strategy to address data scarcity and the underrepresentation of clinically important phenotypes in medical imaging, yet generating images that faithfully reflect meaningful patient characteristics remains challenging. In this work, we investigate metadata-conditioned cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) synthesis using a pretrained latent diffusion model, encoding structured clinical metadata and slice position as textual prompts to guide CMR generation. To improve metadata adherence and address the imbalance of clinical attributes, we integrate three strategies: Metadata-Free Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG), Contrastive Batching, and Inverse-Frequency Sampling. The framework was fine-tuned and evaluated on 59,058 short-axis CMR from the UK Biobank using paired image similarity, distributional fidelity, and subgroup-level analyses. The combined approach achieved a Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) of 37.47, improving by 57.04\% over the same model fine-tuned without these strategies and by 28.68\% over a previous text-conditioned CMR diffusion baseline requiring cardiac geometry as additional input, while relying solely on patient metadata. This distributional gain, driven mainly by Metadata-Free CFG, came with a modest reduction in paired similarity, suggesting that the model prioritizes population-level realism over exact image reproduction. Subgroup analyses demonstrated improved alignment across demographic and acquisition-related metadata, with disease-specific conditioning being the most challenging task. These findings demonstrate the potential of generative foundation models for clinically meaningful CMR synthesis while highlighting the need for more effective metadata-aware conditioning strategies. Our code is available at https://github.com/rodriguezmarc/conditional-cmr.

</details>


### [62] [B-MIM: Biased Masked Image Modeling for Generalizable Segmentation of Fine-Grained Anatomical Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24364)
*Sebastián González,Karen Sanchez,José M. Saavedra,Marcelo Pizarro,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出B-MIM方法，通过随机减少全局语义对齐以优先局部重建，增强CT编码器对精细解剖结构（如血管、小肿瘤）的敏感性，并在多机构数据集上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CT编码器大多偏向于粗粒度的语义理解，导致其对血管或小肿瘤等精细解剖结构的敏感度不足，限制了模型在细微结构捕捉上的表现。

Method: 引入有偏掩码图像建模（B-MIM），这是对iBOT目标的修改，通过随机降低全局语义对齐权重来优先进行局部patch重建。使用9,955个过滤后的腹部CT研究数据预训练3D Swin Transformer骨干网络。

Result: 在肝脏血管分割任务中提高了拓扑保真度（clDice），在肿瘤分割任务中取得了具有竞争力的Dice分数。尽管仅更新部分参数，其性能优于全微调基线模型。

Conclusion: 在预训练阶段减少全局语义压力有助于增强模型对复杂精细解剖结构的泛化能力。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining enables transferable representations for medical imaging, yet most CT encoders remain biased toward coarse semantic understanding, limiting their sensitivity to fine-grained anatomical structures such as vessels or small tumors. In this paper, we introduce Biased Masked Image Modeling (B-MIM), a modification of the iBOT objective that stochastically reduces global semantic alignment to prioritize local patch reconstruction. This bias encourages the encoder to capture high-frequency morphological details and structural continuity. We curate a multi-institutional CT abdominal dataset of 9,955 filtered studies from 17 public sources and pretrain a 3D Swin Transformer backbone using B-MIM. Across inter-dataset experiments on liver vessel segmentation, the proposed encoder improves topological fidelity (clDice) and achieves competitive Dice scores in tumor segmentation, compared to fully fine-tuned baselines, despite updating only a fraction of the parameters. Our results suggest that reducing global semantic pressure during pretraining enhances generalization to intricate anatomical structures.

</details>


### [63] [MaST: Motion-aware Sparse Pipeline for Lightweight Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24365)
*Qingmao Wei,Fagui Liu,Dengke Zhang,Qingze He,Quan Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MaST，一种通过注入运动先验和优化稀疏预测头，实现端到端稀疏Transformer跟踪框架，在保持高精度的同时显著提升边缘设备推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于Transformer的跟踪器因密集token处理导致的计算成本高、难以在边缘设备实时部署的问题；克服现有稀疏化方法因缺乏运动先验导致早期评分噪声大，以及最终需密集重塑以适配预测头从而抵消稀疏收益的局限。

Method: 1. 注入轻量级运动先验以优化基于交叉注意力的token重要性评分，实现搜索区域中更早且更稳定的token剪枝；2. 设计原生稀疏预测头，直接对保留的非结构化token进行“先评分、后回归”操作，消除密集填充/重塑带来的冗余计算。

Result: 在LaSOT上AUC达63.8（超越AsymTrack-S +1.0），TrackingNet上SUC达80.1（超越+2.2）；在Jetson Nano上运行速度达152 FPS，是AsymTrack-S（88 FPS）的近两倍，确立轻量化跟踪器的新SOTA。

Conclusion: MaST通过端到端的稀疏化处理，有效平衡了计算效率与跟踪精度，为边缘设备上的实时视频跟踪提供了高性能解决方案。

Abstract: Transformer-based object trackers are renowned for their strong performance, yet dense token processing often leads to prohibitive computational cost, limiting real-time deployment on edge devices. While recent works explore token pruning to reduce computation, they often stop short of an end-to-end sparse pipeline, as early-layer token scores can be noisy without a motion prior, and many trackers ultimately fall back to dense reshaping to feed the dense prediction head that partially negates the savings.
  We introduce Motion-aware Sparse Tracker (MaST), a sparse tracking framework that makes sparsity effective from tokens to boxes. First, MaST injects a lightweight motion prior to refine cross-attention-based importance scores, enabling earlier and more stable token reduction in the search region. Second, we introduce a natively sparse prediction head that operates directly on the retained unstructured tokens with a score-first, regress-once design, eliminating dense padding/reshaping and reducing redundant computation.
  Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that MaST establishes new state of the art among lightweight trackers, where MaST-tiny attains 63.8 AUC on LaSOT and 80.1 SUC on TrackingNet, surpassing the prior best AsymTrack-S by +1.0 AUC and +2.2 SUC
  while running at 152 FPS on Jetson Nano, nearly twice as fast as AsymTrack-S at 88 FPS. Code is available at https://github.com/TsingWei/MaST.

</details>


### [64] [Variance-Guided Spatial Attention Fusion for Robust End-to-End Driving under Asymmetric Sensor Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24366)
*Weizhi Tao,Zengwang Jin,Xiao Wang,Hailong Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VG-SAF框架，通过异方差不确定性估计和物理增强生成空间注意力门控，解决端到端多模态驾驶在传感器非对称退化下的鲁棒性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端多模态驾驶管线在非对称传感器退化（如整个模态或局部区域受损）下表现脆弱，缺乏对特征可靠性的密集监督及物理故障严重程度的校准机制，导致不可靠特征误导规划器。

Method: 提出方差引导的空间注意力融合（VG-SAF），包含三个组件：1) 基于物理的增强器模拟相机/LiDAR故障并生成连续空间掩码以提供密集监督；2) 模态特定专家通过跨分支密集蒸馏预测像素级可靠性尺度，确保单调的严重程度-尺度响应；3) 校准后的可靠性图驱动混合注意力机制，利用局部空间门控抑制不可靠单元并通过跨模态信任softmax仲裁模态间权重；此外还包含一个Laplace不确定性头用于输出系统级路径点不确定性。

Result: 在CARLA Longest6基准测试中，VG-SAF在仅相机、仅LiDAR及联合退化场景下，相比基线方法在驾驶得分、路线完成率和违规得分方面均持续提升了闭环鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过引入可解释的空间门控和密集可靠性监督，有效解决了传感器退化导致的特征偏差问题，显著增强了自动驾驶系统在复杂故障环境下的稳定性与安全性。

Abstract: End-to-end multimodal driving has progressed rapidly by fusing camera and LiDAR streams. Existing pipelines remain fragile under asymmetric sensor degradation, where either an entire modality or only a localized region is corrupted while other regions remain useful. The key difficulty is not simply to add an uncertainty head, but to obtain dense reliability supervision, calibrate this reliability against physical fault severity, and use it before unreliable features bias the planner. We propose Variance-Guided Spatial Attention Fusion (VG-SAF), in which dense heteroscedastic reliability estimates act as interpretable spatial gates. The framework couples three components. First, a physically grounded augmentor simulates representative camera and LiDAR failures and emits a continuous spatial mask, providing dense supervision without additional annotation. Second, modality-specific experts predict per-pixel reliability scales through cross-branch dense distillation in log space, enforcing a monotone severity-to-scale response. Third, calibrated reliability maps drive a hybrid attention mechanism that suppresses unreliable cells with a local spatial gate and arbitrates between modalities through a cross-modal trust softmax. A Laplace uncertainty head emits a systemic waypoint uncertainty scale that signals severe or combined sensor degradation, including severities outside the training ranges. On the CARLA Longest6 benchmark, VG-SAF consistently improves closed-loop robustness over the baselines across camera-only, LiDAR-only, and joint degradation regimes, as measured by driving score, route completion, and infraction score.

</details>


### [65] [Bridging Adversarial and Collaborative Learning for AI-Generated Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24372)
*Baoliang Chen,Qing Lin,Sijie Mai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种交互感知学习框架，通过门控模块动态调节感知保真度与提示对齐之间的竞争/合作关系，以模拟人类评分的依赖性，提升AIGIQA准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGIQA模型通常将感知保真度和提示对齐视为独立维度，但研究发现人类评分中这两者存在相互依赖及竞争/合作互动，需构建能自适应协商二者关系的统一模型。

Method: 引入交互感知学习框架，包含对抗性与协作性推理路径；设计门控交互模块，根据推断的关系动态路由特征；利用任务感知提示调制门控行为，实现竞争与合作模式的切换。

Result: 在多个AIGIQA基准测试上达到最先进精度，并产生可解释的交互模式，更忠实地近似人类判断。

Conclusion: 该模型通过动态调节感知与对齐的相互作用，提供了比传统方法更符合人类直觉的质量评估方案。

Abstract: AI-generated image quality assessment (AIGIQA) requires jointly reasoning about perceptual fidelity and prompt alignment, two quality dimensions that are often treated as independent in existing AIGIQA models. However, by re-examining human ratings, we uncover a previously overlooked phenomenon: the two dimensions are interdependent and exhibit both competitive and cooperative interactions during human rating. This observation suggests that a unified model should neither collapse the two dimensions nor rigidly separate them, but rather adaptively negotiate their interplay. Motivated by this insight, we introduce an interaction-aware learning framework that models perception-alignment relations through adversarial and collaborative inference pathways. Instead of designing a rigid dual-branch architecture, our method employs a gated interaction module that dynamically routes features according to the inferred relationship between the two dimensions. Task-aware prompts further modulate the gating behaviour, enabling the model to switch between competition and cooperation when necessary. Experiments across multiple AIGIQA benchmarks demonstrate that our approach not only achieves state-of-the-art accuracy but also yields interpretable interaction patterns, offering a more faithful approximation of human judgment. The codes are available at https://github.com/LQAMEI/ACL-IQA.

</details>


### [66] [Markerless Pose Estimation for Resistance Training Technique Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24384)
*Joseph Turner,Jeff Clark,Nawid Keshtmand*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于普通视频的无标记姿态估计框架，用于评估深蹲、卧推和硬拉等抗阻训练技术。通过BlazePose提取解剖学标志点并计算关节角度轨迹，利用均方根误差（RMSE）与参考动作进行对比。结果显示该方法能恢复有意义的运动学模式，支持定量比较和技术变异性识别，但性能受相机视角和遮挡影响较大。结论表明无标记姿态估计可作为实验室外生物力学评估的可行替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗阻训练存在较高受伤风险，正确的动作形式至关重要。传统的基于实验室的运动分析虽能提供量化技术评估，但可及性差。无标记姿态估计无需物理标记即可从图像或视频中推断身体标志点，为技术评估提供了一种可行的替代方案。

Method: 构建了一个姿态估计框架，使用BlazePose算法从深蹲、卧推和硬拉的普通视频中提取解剖学标志点，并将其转换为关节角度轨迹。以深蹲为主要案例研究，将生成的轨迹与定义的标准重复动作进行对比，采用均方根误差（RMSE）进行评估。

Result: 该框架成功恢复了深蹲和硬拉中有意义的运动学模式，实现了重复动作间的定量比较以及组内技术变异性的识别。然而，性能强烈依赖于相机方向和视觉遮挡情况，非矢状面视角会导致2D关节角度估计失真。

Conclusion: 无标记姿态估计能够支持在非实验室环境下进行可及的生物力学评估，为抗阻训练技术的现场量化分析提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Resistance training can be a high risk activity, and safe form is essential to avoiding injury. Laboratory-based movement analysis provides quantitive technique assessment, yet is not easily accessible. Markerless pose estimation infers body landmarks from images or video without physical markers and could offer a feasible alternative for technique assessment. We present a pose estimation framework to evaluate resistance-training technique from ordinary video footage. Using BlazePose, anatomical landmarks were extracted from squat, bench press, and deadlift videos and converted into joint-angle trajectories, with the squat serving as the primary case study. Trajectories were assessed against a defined reference repetition using root mean square error (RMSE). Results show that the framework recovers meaningful kinematic patterns for the squat and deadlift, enabling quantitative comparison between repetitions and identification of technique variability within a set. Performance depended strongly on camera orientation and visual occlusion, with non-sagittal views distorting 2D joint-angle estimates. The findings demonstrate that markerless pose estimation can support accessible biomechanical assessment outside laboratory environments.

</details>


### [67] [MRI-based Deep Radiomic Phenotyping of Neuromuscular Disorders: A Topology-driven Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24415)
*Martyna Żur,Łukasz Piórecki,Marek Socha,Jordi Diaz-Manera,Jose Verdu Diaz,Volker Straub,Rossella Tupler,Joanna Polańska*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于原始特征的自动化放射组学表型分析框架，利用CoMPaSS-NMD项目的1184例肌肉MRI扫描数据，通过定量形态测量、空间分布、几何形状、脂肪替代阶段相互作用及基于图论的拓扑结构五个领域，将异质性肌内脂质变性映射为可解释的生物标志物。研究发现基于图的脂肪浸润骨架化提取的拓扑网络指标（如SF1_Skel_Nodes）和界面动力学指标具有显著的判别力（效应量较大），优于传统体积指标，能捕捉疾病特异性宏观浸润模式。该自动特征提取流程已集成至MUSCAT库中，证明全局架构特征是神经肌肉疾病差异诊断和纵向监测的高潜力生物标志物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量评估肌肉MRI对于监测神经肌肉疾病（NMD）至关重要。传统方法多依赖空间无关的体积指标，难以全面反映复杂的3D肌肉架构变化，因此需要开发能够量化复杂肌内脂质变性三维结构的新型生物标志物。

Method: 构建了一个基于五大主要架构领域的原始特征工程自动化放射组学表型框架：定量形态测量、空间分布、几何形状、渐进性脂肪替代阶段的相互作用以及基于图论的拓扑结构。使用CoMPaSS-NMD项目的1184例MRI扫描数据，引入基于图的脂肪浸润骨架化技术以量化肌肉架构变化，建立跨越整个3D肌肉体积的拓扑网络映射。采用非参数Kruskal-Wallis分析进行统计筛选，并结合事后成对评估和UMAP投影验证特征效能。

Result: 统计筛选证实了这些新型描述符在遗传层级上的判别能力。拓扑网络指标（如SF1_Skel_Nodes, $ε^2$ = 0.2656）和界面动力学指标（如SF2_To_SF1_Dist_Min, $ε^2$ = 0.2092）显示出 substantial effect sizes（显著效应量），提供了比经典体积评估更深层的结构洞察。UMAP投影表明这些拓扑和3D几何不变量能够捕获疾病特异性的宏观浸润模式。

Conclusion: 全局架构特征是一类极具前景的生物标志物，可用于神经肌肉疾病的差异诊断及追踪纵向疾病动态。开发的自动化特征提取流程已整合并公开于MUSCAT (MUSCle fAt Topology) 库中。

Abstract: Quantitative assessment of muscle MRI is crucial for monitoring neuromuscular disorders (NMD). This study introduces an automated radiomic phenotyping framework based on original features engineered across five main architectural domains: quantitative morphometry, spatial distribution, geometric shape, interactions between progressive fat replacement stages, and graph-based topology. Utilizing 1184 MRI scans from the CoMPaSS-NMD project, we map the complex 3D architecture of heterogeneous intramuscular lipodegeneration into objective, morphologically interpretable biomarkers. We introduce a graph-based skeletonization of fat infiltrates to quantify muscle architectural changes, establishing a multi-dimensional extension of traditional, spatially-agnostic volume metrics by mapping topological networks across the entire 3D muscle volume. Statistical screening via non-parametric Kruskal-Wallis analysis confirmed the discriminative power of these novel descriptors across the genetic hierarchy. Notably, topological network metrics (e.g., SF1_Skel_Nodes, $ε^2$ = 0.2656) and interface dynamics metrics (e.g., SF2_To_SF1_Dist_Min, $ε^2$ = 0.2092) demonstrated substantial effect sizes, providing deeper structural insights than classical volumetric assessments. Post-hoc pairwise evaluations and UMAP projections further indicated the capability of these topological and 3D geometric invariants to capture disease-specific macroscopic infiltration patterns. These results demonstrate that global architectural features represent a highly promising class of biomarkers for differential diagnosis, offering new avenues for tracking longitudinal disease dynamics in neuromuscular diagnostics. The developed automated feature extraction pipeline is integrated and available within the MUSCAT (MUSCle fAt Topology) library.

</details>


### [68] [ZODIAC: Zero-shot Octree-based Diffusion for Anatomical Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24422)
*Miruna-Alexandra Gafencu,Vlad Bratulescu,Yordanka Velikova,Mohammad Farid Azampour,Nassir Navab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种零样本形状补全框架ZODIAC，利用扩散先验和自适应八叉树结构，无需模拟训练数据即可从部分超声图像重建完整腰椎3D解剖结构，显著提升了真实遮挡下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的监督式超声形状补全方法依赖合成数据，假设遮挡遵循特定分布，导致在真实术中不规则、不可见的遮挡模式下泛化能力受限，无法解决从噪声部分观测中准确恢复完整脊柱的问题。

Method: 提出零样本形状补全框架，引入“混合补全”机制，在推理时将学习到的解剖先验与输入的部分几何信息结合；采用生成式扩散先验建模自适应八叉树结构中的完整解剖形状，实现单次前向传播高效重建。

Result: 在体模和志愿者数据上的验证表明，该方法解耦了补全过程与预定义的损坏分布，在真实遮挡下泛化能力更强，HD95补全误差比完全监督变体降低了22%。

Conclusion: 通过摒弃对模拟数据的依赖并引入灵活的零样本推断机制，该框架有效解决了真实术中超声遮挡导致的脊柱形态重建难题，为临床精准导航提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Recovering the full 3D spine anatomy from intraoperative ultrasound is an ill-posed inverse problem, as the complete structure must be inferred from incomplete and noisy observations. Acoustic occlusions and limited field of view create large unobserved regions, while view-dependent artifacts lead to variability in expert annotations of the visible anatomy. Current supervised ultrasound shape completion methods rely on synthetically generated incomplete-complete paired data to learn conditional mappings under a predefined distribution of simulated occlusions. However, real intraoperative occlusions do not necessarily follow this distribution, which can limit generalization to patient data. As a result, accurate and robust completion from noisy partial observations remains an unsolved problem. We propose a zero-shot shape completion framework that reconstructs the entire lumbar spine from partial ultrasound observations without relying on simulated training data. To accommodate unseen and irregular patterns of missing structures, we introduce blended completion, a mechanism that integrates the learned anatomical prior with incoming partial geometry at inference time. The method learns a generative diffusion prior over full anatomical shapes represented in an adaptive octree structure, enabling efficient modeling of the complete spine in a single forward pass. Validation on phantom and volunteer data shows that decoupling completion from a predefined corruption distribution improves generalisation under real occlusions, outperforming a fully supervised variant by 22% on HD95 completion error. Code and data are available at https://github.com/miruna20/ZODIAC.

</details>


### [69] [Vision Language Model Fusion for Explainable Face Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24430)
*Ana Estrada-Real,Lydia Alapatt,Christoph Busch,Christian Rathgeb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种多视觉语言模型（VLM）融合框架，通过整合多个模型的相似度分数、文本解释和面部图像，不仅提升了人脸识别的准确率，还增强了决策的可解释性和透明度，实现了更负责任的人脸验证系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负责任的人脸验证系统不仅需要准确的决策，还需要提供可解释且可审计的证据。单一VLM模型在决策准确性和解释质量上存在局限，因此需要探索结合多个VLM以提升性能并丰富解释内容。

Method: 评估了四个独立的VLM作为人脸验证系统，并提出一个融合框架：两个源模型提供相似度分数和文本理由，第三个VLM作为决策者。设计了四种融合场景，逐步向决策者输入分数、理由、面部图像及其组合。

Result: 多VLM融合不仅提高了识别性能，还提供了互补的解释视角。提出的多模态VLM结合了决策分数、解释和面部图像，其识别准确率优于最先进的VLM和领域专用模型，且生成的融合解释更具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 多VLM融合有助于开发负责任的可解释人脸验证系统，使用户不仅能理解最终决策，还能掌握背后的证据和潜在来源，从而支持更高的透明度、可审计性和错误分析能力。

Abstract: Responsible deployment of face verification systems requires more than accurate decisions: systems should also provide interpretable and auditable evidence that enables users to understand, assess, and challenge their decisions. Vision-language models (VLMs) provide a promising foundation for explainable face recognition by combining visual analysis with natural-language reasoning. However, relying on a single model may further limit the decision accuracy as well as provided explanations. This work therefore investigates whether multiple VLMs can be combined to improve recognition accuracy, and to enrich the explanations associated with those decisions. This work evaluates four VLMs as standalone face verification systems and subsequently proposes a fusion framework, where two source models provide similarity scores and textual justifications and a third VLM acts as a decider model. Four different fusion scenarios are considered, progressively providing the decider model with scores, justifications, face images, and combinations of these modalities.
  Overall, the findings suggest that the value of multi-VLM fusion extends beyond recognition performance. VLMs can provide complementary justifications and perspectives that enable richer explanations of face recognition decisions, supporting greater transparency, auditability, and error analysis. This is relevant to the development of responsible explainable face verification systems, where users and operators should be able to understand not only the final decision but also the evidence and potential sources underlying it. The proposed multimodal VLM, which combines decision scores, explanations, and face images, achieves higher recognition accuracy than state-of-the-art VLMs and domain-specific face recognition models, while also providing fused explanations that are expected to be more robust than those generated by individual VLMs.

</details>


### [70] [DoublesEval: Diagnosing Multi-Agent Tactical Reasoning in Vision-Language Models via Professional Doubles Badminton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24439)
*Jintao Cheng,Weibin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DoublesEval评估框架和TacticCheck推理优化方法，旨在解决视觉语言模型（VLMs）在双打羽毛球场景中多智能体战术推理能力的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLMs擅长描述静态场景，但在处理依赖角色协调和时空依赖的动态多智能体交互推理方面存在显著缺陷。

Method: 1. 构建DoublesEval：以双打羽毛球为测试床，通过关键瞬间分解协议，从原子识别、片段内理解、跨片段因果推理和高阶战术抽象四个维度诊断模型能力；2. 提出TacticCheck：一种轻量级约束引导的测试时一致性检查器，利用模型自身的低级战术预测对候选答案重排序，无需参数更新或标签。

Result: 在60个精心策划的双打回合（约9.6K结构化实例）上评估了4个开源VLMs，发现模型在所有诊断层级均表现薄弱，瓶颈在于空间状态、交互绑定和终端证据。TacticCheck在所有模型上带来一致的性能提升，但与鲁棒的战术推理相比仍有巨大差距。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前VLMs在多智能体交互推理上的局限性，强调了开发结构化、交互感知评估范式的重要性，以促进下一代VLM的发展。

Abstract: Visual Language Models (VLMs) excel at describing visible scene content but struggle to reason about dynamic multi-agent interactions, where action semantics depend on coordinated roles and spatial-temporal dependencies. We formalize this capability as \textbf{multi-agent tactical reasoning} and introduce \textbf{DoublesEval}, a diagnostic evaluation framework that leverages professional doubles badminton as a structurally tractable testbed. DoublesEval employs a key-moment-based protocol that decomposes rallies into tactically salient instants and probes models across four interpretable dimensions: atomic recognition, intra-segment composite understanding, cross-segment causal reasoning, and high-level tactical abstraction. This design isolates \emph{where} reasoning fails, rather than merely measuring answer correctness. To address observed failure modes, we propose \textbf{TacticCheck}, a lightweight constraint-guided test-time consistency checker that reranks candidate answers using the model's own lower-level tactical predictions, requiring no parameter updates or ground-truth labels at inference time. Evaluating four representative open-source VLMs on 60 curated rallies (yielding $\sim$9.6K structured instances) via a zero-shot protocol, we find that models remain weak across all diagnostic levels, with especially clear bottlenecks in spatial state, interaction binding, and terminal evidence. TacticCheck delivers consistent gains across all evaluated models, while still leaving a substantial gap to robust tactical reasoning. These results highlight the need for structured, interaction-aware evaluation paradigms for next-generation VLMs. The source code is available in \href{https://github.com/Chengjt1999/DoublesEval}{\textcolor{blue}{our GitHub repository}}.

</details>
