Motivation: 尽管基于文本的幻觉检测研究广泛，但针对低资源语言的口语幻觉检测仍 largely unexplored（很大程度上未被探索）。

Method: 1. 构建包含12,013个新闻样本的多语种口语幻觉基准（英、俄、哈萨克语），涵盖三种类型和三个严重程度的受控幻觉；2. 补充收集290条原生事实核查后的假新闻（俄/哈语）；3. 使用TTS-ASR流水线生成对齐的文本和音频数据；4. 评估微调的多语言编码器及零样本多模态解码器在基于转录本与直接音频处理上的表现。

Result: 1. 基于转录本的检测通常优于直接音频处理；2. 强编码器的二分类任务性能下降与特定语言的ASR错误率相关；3. 在真实假新闻上，由合成数据训练的探测器表现出强迁移能力（宏观F1为0.82-0.88）；4. 俄语溯源分析揭示了真实性相关和模型依赖的机器风格信号，量化了合成幻觉基准中的关键混淆因素。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了多语种口语幻觉检测的空白，证明了合成数据训练的有效性，同时指出了当前基准中存在的机器生成信号作为混淆变量的问题，为未来研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: While text-based hallucination detection has been extensively studied, spoken hallucination detection remains largely unexplored, particularly for low-resource languages. We present the first multilingual spoken hallucination benchmark comprising 12,013 news samples across English, Russian, and Kazakh with controlled hallucinations of three types and three severity levels. Samples comprise original articles and aligned hallucinated counterparts in text and audio. We complement the synthetic corpus with 290 fact-checked fake news items collected natively in Russian (225) and Kazakh (65), translated into the other language and rendered through the same TTS-ASR pipeline. We assess fine-tuned multilingual encoders and, in zero-shot in-context settings, multimodal decoder models on transcript-based versus direct audio processing. Transcript-based detection generally outperforms direct audio processing, with binary-task degradation for strong encoders tracking per-language ASR error. On real-world fakes, synthetic-trained detectors transfer strongly (macro-F1 0.82-0.88 on original text), while Russian provenance analysis reveals both veracity-related and model-dependent machine-style signals, quantifying a key confound in synthetic hallucination benchmarks.

</details>


### [143] [Arbitrary Polygon Oscillator: Generalizing Polygonal Synthesis to Arbitrary Shapes, Morphing, and Three-Dimensional Polyhedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24726)
*Antonio Argentieri,Francesco Scagliola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用的多边形合成系统，突破了传统仅适用于规则多边形的限制。通过统一弧长引擎、混合插值算法及三维扩展，实现了任意形状（包括不规则和星形）的多边形波形生成与平滑变形，并结合RNBO平台优化了性能与抗锯齿效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多边形的音频合成方法通常局限于具有恒定角速度的规则参数化多边形，导致音色转换受限且无法处理顶点数不等或形状复杂的多边形。旨在打破这一局限，实现更广泛、更灵活的多边形波形生成。

Method: 1. 构建统一的弧长引擎，允许任意来源的顶点数据输入DSP流水线；2. 开发混合插值算法，支持顶点数不等的多边形之间进行平滑变形，生成无参数描述的中间形状；3. 将合成范式扩展至三维，通过旋转凸多面体并由固定平面切片获取可变截面多边形；4. 在RNBO中实现几何缓存策略以避免逐样本重算，并采用基于运行时贝塞尔切线的四点polyBLAMP校正结合自适应过采样进行抗锯齿处理。

Result: 成功实现了任意闭合多边形（规则、不规则或星形）作为波形发生器；支持不同顶点数量多边形间的平滑形态学演变；引入了三维多面体切片控制二维多边形的新型合成维度；系统在实时音频环境中高效运行，具备良好的音质和计算效率。

Conclusion: 该研究通过统一弧长引擎和通用化处理，极大地扩展了多边形合成的应用范围，为电子音乐创作提供了更丰富的音色可能性和更自然的形状过渡能力，同时证明了其在实时DSP环境中的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Polygonal synthesis generates audio by traversing the perimeter of a polygon with a phasor; prior work uses a constant angular velocity, whereas the proposed system adopts constant arc-length (perimeter) velocity. Existing formulations operate on regular, parametrically defined polygons, producing smooth timbral transitions within a single family of shapes.
  This paper generalizes polygonal synthesis around a unified arc-length engine: vertex data of any origin feed the same DSP pipeline. First, we adapt the oscillator to accept arbitrary vertex configurations from an external buffer, opening the possibility for a broad class of closed polygons -- regular, irregular, or star-shaped -- to function as a waveform generator. Second, a hybrid interpolation algorithm enables smooth morphing between polygons with unequal vertex counts, passing through intermediate shapes that have no parametric description. Third, we extend the paradigm to three dimensions: a convex polyhedron rotated about three axes is sliced by a fixed horizontal plane, and the resulting cross-section yields a continuously variable polygon controlled by the solid's orientation. The system runs in RNBO (Cycling~'74) with a geometry caching strategy that avoids per-sample recomputation. Antialiasing combines a four-point polyBLAMP correction derived from runtime Bézier tangents with adaptive oversampling, adapting the correction geometrically to general vertex configurations without per-shape analytical derivation.

</details>


### [144] [SkillForge: Evolving Verifiable Skills for Reinforcement Learning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24747)
*Shidong Yang,Ziyu Ma,Tongwen Huang,Xucong Wang,Renda Li,Yiming Hu,Yong Wang,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SkillForge框架，通过环境交互验证和精炼技能，解决LLM智能体在强化学习中无法积累可复用知识的问题，显著提升复杂决策任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习（RL）训练的大语言模型（LLM）智能体多为单集式（episodic），难以跨集积累可复用知识；现有技能提取方法（如SkillRL）将技能库视为只增不减的存储库，缺乏对已存技能有效性的验证机制。

Method: 提出SkillForge框架，实现技能的持续进化：1. 显式化技能使用，使RL能直接优化环境动作和技能调用决策；2. 引入基于证据的技能验证机制，确保技能质量；3. 采用多路径技能诱导，使技能库在持续增长的同时保持高质量。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和AppWorld上的广泛实验表明，SkillForge consistently优于SkillRL，证明了持续验证技能在训练更强LLM智能体方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过环境交互对技能进行持续验证和精炼，可以有效解决LLM智能体技能复用和质量维护问题，从而提升其在复杂决策任务中的表现。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are trained with reinforcement learning (RL) for complex decision-making tasks. However, most RL-trained agents remain episodic and cannot accumulate reusable knowledge across episodes. Recent skill-based approaches, such as SkillRL, attempt to address this issue by extracting skills from raw trajectories, but treat the skill bank as an append-only repository without verifying whether stored skills remain effective. In this paper, we propose SkillForge, a framework for continuous skill evolution that enables skills to be verified and refined through environment interaction. By making skill usage explicit during agent interaction, RL can directly optimize both environment actions and skill invocation decisions. SkillForge further introduces evidence-based skill verification and multi-pathway skill induction, allowing the skill bank to continuously grow while maintaining its quality. Extensive experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and AppWorld show that SkillForge consistently outperforms SkillRL, demonstrating the effectiveness of continuously verified skills in training stronger LLM agents.

</details>


### [145] [The RAT: A Unified Bayesian Model for RAG Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24753)
*Pius von Däniken,Felix Matthias Saaro,Mark Cieliebak,Jan Deriu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种贝叶斯评估框架，用于评估检索增强生成（RAG）系统，通过联合建模检索成功、拒绝回答行为和答案正确性，揭示不同配置在条件分解下的行为差异，并整合LLM-as-a-judge标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG评估主要关注端到端的正确性，但忽略了组件间的相互作用和错误传播机制，无法充分反映系统在具体任务流程中的实际表现。

Method: 构建一个基于信息流的贝叶斯概率模型，对检索成功、生成器适当性及最终答案正确性进行因子分解；实验涵盖27种RAG配置及多个数据集/模型；分析标注分配问题以证明检索成功标注的信息价值；引入校准的噪声观测机制，将LLM-as-a-judge作为廉价自动评估融入统一模型。

Result: 条件分解揭示了在边际指标下看似等效的系统之间存在显著的行为差异；证明了检索成功标注比任务成功标注更能有效估计策略遵循情况；实现了人类有限判断与低成本自动化评估的结合。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了更细粒度的RAG系统评估视角，不仅提升了评估的准确性，还通过结合LLM标注降低了人工成本，为优化RAG流水线提供了理论依据和实践工具。

Abstract: Evaluating Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems requires assessing not only end-to-end correctness but also how individual components interact and how errors propagate through the pipeline. We introduce a Bayesian evaluation framework that jointly models retrieval success, abstention behavior, and answer correctness, factorized according to the pipeline's information flow. The model distinguishes task success. Whether the user received a correct answer (from generator success) and whether the generator behaved appropriately given the retrieval outcome. We apply the framework to 27 RAG configurations across three datasets, three retrievers, and three generators, and show that the conditional decomposition reveals substantial behavioral differences between systems that appear equivalent under marginal metrics. We further analyze the annotation allocation problem, demonstrating that retrieval-success annotations are more informative than task-success annotations for estimating policy adherence, and provide an information-theoretic explanation for this asymmetry. Finally, we extend the model to incorporate LLM-as-a-judge annotations as calibrated noisy observations, enabling practitioners to combine limited human judgments with cheaper automated assessments within a unified probabilistic model.

</details>


### [146] [ExpConCAD: Experience-Guided Text-to-CAD Generation from Shape Descriptions with Implicit Spatial Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24760)
*Jingyao Liu,Jinkang Tang,Chen Huang,Wenqiang Lei,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ExpConCAD框架，通过恢复构建结构并检索相关设计经验，自动补全自然语言描述中缺失的空间约束，从而生成可执行的CAD程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的自然语言描述通常是不完整的，缺少构建有效CAD模型所需的关键空间约束，而现有方法大多忽视了这一挑战。

Method: 提出ExpConCAD框架：首先恢复预期的构建结构和约束范围；其次为相似的约束范围检索相关的约束补全经验；最后生成可执行的CadQuery程序。

Result: 大量实验证明了ExpConCAD的有效性，并揭示了构建结构理解和经验记忆在空间约束补全中的作用。

Conclusion: 基于底层构建结构和可复用设计经验来推断缺失的空间约束是解决文本到CAD生成中描述不完整问题的有效途径。

Abstract: Text-to-CAD aims to generate executable CAD programs from natural-language descriptions. However, real-world descriptions are often underspecified and omit critical spatial constraints required for valid CAD construction, a challenge that has been largely overlooked by existing methods. In this paper, we argue that missing spatial constraints should be inferred with respect to the underlying construction structure and informed by reusable design experience. Based on this insight, we propose ExpConCAD, an experience-enhanced framework for implicit spatial constraint completion. ExpConCAD first recovers the intended construction structure and constraint scopes, then retrieves relevant constraint-completion experience for similar scopes to complete the missing spatial constraints, and finally generates executable CadQuery programs. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of ExpConCAD and provide insights into the role of construction structure understanding and experience memory in spatial constraint completion. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Hotjiashell/ExpConCAD.

</details>


### [147] [Linear Probing Provides Robust and Efficient Detection of Machine-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24780)
*Gerrit Quaremba,Hanqi Yan,Elizabeth Black,Denny Vrandecic,Elena Simperl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现机器生成文本（MGT）与人类写作文本（HWT）的潜在表示在低维空间中具有线性可分性，提出简单的线性探针作为检测器。该方法不仅泛化能力更强（OOD检测AUC提升11），且仅需不到100个样本即可达到接近峰值性能，显著优于现有基准模型，并揭示了“机器程度”的连续谱系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的监督式MGT检测器通常面临域外（OOD）泛化能力下降的问题，且需要大量多样化的训练数据。为了克服这一限制，研究旨在探索更简单、样本效率更高且鲁棒的检测方法。

Method: 分析MGT和HWT潜在表示的线性分离性和质量差异。基于此，训练两种变体的简单线性探针，并在4个基准测试中将其与16种基线方法进行对比评估，重点考察其跨域泛化能力和样本效率。

Result: 1. MGT和HWT的潜在表示在低维空间中是线性可分的，这源于两者表示质量的系统性差异。
2. 线性探针在OOD检测任务上表现优异，AUC提升11个点。
3. 样本效率极高，仅需少于100个样本即可达到接近最佳的性能。
4. 探针能够恢复出跨多种设置通用的共享潜在MGT方向，证明了其良好的迁移性。
5. 探针向量能捕捉“机器程度”的连续谱，适用于AI编辑文本的细粒度估计。

Conclusion: 线性探针作为一种稳健且样本高效的MGT检测器具有巨大潜力。本研究揭示了MGT与HWT在潜在空间中的本质差异，并为开发无需大规模数据的通用检测器提供了新见解。代码已开源。

Abstract: Distinguishing machine-generated text (MGT) from human-written text (HWT) becomes increasingly important due to potential misuse. However, most supervised detectors often degrade out-of-domain (OOD) and require large, diverse training sets. In this work, we analyze the linearity and quality of MGT representations and show that simple linear probes outperform a wide range of detectors while being substantially more sample-efficient. We first show that MGT and HWT latent representations are linearly separable in low-dimensional space, and provide a plausible explanation for this separability through systematic differences in their representation quality. Motivated by these insights, we train two variants of simple linear probes and evaluate them across 4 benchmarks against 16 baselines. Probes consistently improve OOD detection (+11 AUC), requiring solely ${<}100$ samples to reach near-peak performance. We show that this transferability arises because probes recover a shared latent MGT direction that generalizes across diverse settings. Finally, we demonstrate that probing vectors capture a continuous spectrum of ``machineness'', highlighting their potential for fine-grained estimation of AI-edited text. Overall, our work provides insights into latent-space differences between MGT and HWT and demonstrates the potential of linear probes as as robust and sample-efficient MGT detectors. We release our code on~\href{https://github.com/gerritq/mgt_probes}{github}.

</details>


### [148] [Reading Is Not Using: Retrieval, Judgment, and the Design of AI Financial Research Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24842)
*Miao Liu,Zhizhe Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在金融分析中存在“检索-整合差距”：尽管能准确检索信息，但无关上下文增加会削弱其对投资决策的影响。通过结构化工作流而非简单的分块摘要，可恢复信息对判断的有效性，表明AI分析师性能由模型能力和工作流架构共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM评估主要关注其检索能力，而忽视了检索到的信息是否真正影响了最终的投资判断。研究旨在识别并解决长上下文金融分析中的这一缺陷。

Method: 固定焦点公司信息，仅改变无关上下文长度（2,000至128,000 token），测试风险披露对投资判断的影响；跨模型家族和任务进行复现；使用因果记忆干预技术；对比不同工作流架构（如分块摘要 vs. 针对性结构化重述）。

Result: 随着无关上下文增加，风险披露对投资判断的影响力降至实验噪声水平，尽管直接检索依然准确；更强大的模型仅推迟而非消除此差距；因果干预显示压缩摘要与源文本查找需协同作用；结构化重述工作流能有效恢复信息影响力，而分块摘要则导致信息丢失。

Conclusion: 仅基于检索能力的评估会误导地认证那些实际上忽略已检索信息的系统。AI分析师的性能是模型能力与工作流架构共同作用的结果，设计良好的结构化工作流对于确保信息有效整合至关重要。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as AI analysts to process financial disclosures and support AI-assisted investment decisions. Yet such systems are usually evaluated by what they can retrieve, not whether retrieved information affects their judgments. We identify a retrieval-integration gap in long-context financial analysis. Holding focal-firm information fixed and varying only unrelated context from 2,000 to 128,000 tokens, we find that a risk disclosure's influence on investment judgments falls to the experimental noise floor even as direct retrieval remains accurate. The pattern replicates across model families and judgment tasks and in experiments removing real disclosures from actual 10-K filings. More capable models postpone but do not eliminate the gap. Causal memory interventions show that compressed summaries and source-text lookup jointly transmit disclosures into judgments. Workflow architecture determines whether this transmission succeeds: chunk-and-summarize pipelines evict relevant information, whereas a targeted, structured restatement adjacent to the decision restores its influence. AI analyst performance is therefore jointly determined by model capability and workflow architecture. Retrieval-based evaluations can certify systems whose investment judgments ignore information they demonstrably retrieved.

</details>


### [149] [BrowserForge: Scaling Web Episode via Parallel Browser Sandboxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24848)
*Fei Tang,Huawen Shen,Zhiqiong Lu,Zhengxi Lu,Pengyuan Lyu,Chengquan Zhang,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出BrowserForge框架，通过并行驱动沙箱浏览器在开放网络上大规模生成网页交互轨迹数据，解决了现有数据集规模小、网站覆盖窄的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Web Agent训练依赖高质量交互轨迹，但公开数据集仅包含数千条来自固定且狭窄网站集合的数据，自动化合成管道也受限于预定义站点列表，导致Agent可见的Distinct网站数量增长有限，难以规模化产生多样化数据。

Method: 构建BrowserForge框架，包含三个核心组件：1）开放网络获取阶段，使Agent接触数十万个真实可访问网站；2）沙箱集群管理器，高效调度数百个并发浏览器；3）提议者-求解者双智能体循环（Proposer-Solver），将原始页面转化为可执行任务并收集验证后的轨迹。此外，采用规则加模型清洗管线去除失败运行并将推理重写为统一的思维链格式。最终训练的Agent仅基于截图操作，不使用辅助结构信息。

Result: 生成的语料库包含203,238条轨迹，每条来自不同网站，规模和多样性均超越 prior datasets。在紧凑多模态模型上微调后，在线Online-Mind2Web成功率从25.66%提升至33.33%，静态Multimodal-Mind2Web的步骤准确率也持续提高，且收益随语料库扩展而增加。控制分析证实开放网络获取和广泛网站覆盖是性能提升的关键因素。

Conclusion: 通过大规模开放网络数据采集和高效并行处理，可以显著提升Web Agent的性能和泛化能力，证明了多样化真实世界数据对训练纯视觉驱动Agent的重要性。

Abstract: Web agents that act from rendered pixels avoid the fragility and heavy token cost of reading a page's HTML or accessibility tree, but training them depends on large amounts of high-quality interaction trajectories, and how to produce such data at scale remains an open problem. Public datasets typically contain only a few thousand trajectories drawn from a fixed and narrow set of websites, and even recent automated synthesis pipelines stay bound to predefined site lists or tutorial sources, so the number of distinct websites the agent ever sees barely grows. We present BrowserForge, a framework that generates web interaction data at scale by driving many browser sandboxes in parallel over the open web. BrowserForge couples three components: an open-web sourcing stage that exposes the agent to hundreds of thousands of real, openly reachable websites; a sandbox cluster manager that schedules hundreds of concurrent browsers with high utilization; and a Proposer-Solver dual-agent loop that turns a raw page into an executable task and then collects a verified trajectory for it. A rule-plus-model cleaning pipeline removes failed runs and rewrites the surviving reasoning into a single unified chain-of-thought style. Page structure such as the accessibility tree is used only as a synthesis-time signal; the agent we train and release acts purely from the screenshot. The resulting corpus contains 203,238 trajectories, each collected from a distinct website, larger and more diverse than prior trajectory datasets. Fine-tuning a compact multimodal model on this corpus raises its success rate on the live Online-Mind2Web from 25.66% to 33.33% and consistently improves step accuracy on the static Multimodal-Mind2Web, with the gain growing as the corpus scales. Controlled analyses further confirm that open-web sourcing and broad website coverage are key contributors to the observed improvement.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [SQLite is Enough. Lexical, Semantic, and Hybrid Search with scrydb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24060)
*Timo Breuer*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: scrydb是一个Python库，结合SQLite的FTS5（词法搜索）和sqlite-vec（语义搜索），支持混合检索、重排序与结果融合，为信息检索提供轻量级解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在提供一个轻量级的Python工具，能够在SQLite中同时实现词法、语义及混合搜索，以解决下游信息检索（IR）或智能体搜索任务中的检索需求。

Method: 1. 词法搜索：利用SQLite的全文本搜索扩展FTS5；2. 语义搜索：基于sqlite-vec向量搜索扩展；3. 混合策略：允许用户对检索结果进行重排序和融合，结合上述两种方法。

Result: 在多种IR基准数据集上验证了scrydb的有效性，证明了其在关键词匹配、语义相似性及秩融合方面的性能，并分析了查询延迟及效率与效果之间的权衡。

Conclusion: scrydb提供了一种高效且有效的轻量级检索方案，适用于需要结合词法和语义信息的IR及智能体应用，且代码开源（MIT协议）。

Abstract: This work introduces scrydb, a Python library that enables lexical, semantic, and hybrid search within SQLite. For lexical search, scrydb leverages SQLite's full-text search extension FTS5. Semantic search builds on sqlite-vec, a SQLite extension for vector search. Furthermore, the library allows users to rerank and fuse retrieval results to combine both lexical and semantic approaches, providing a lightweight solution for downstream tasks in information retrieval (IR) or agentic search. We evaluate scrydb on various IR benchmark datasets and demonstrate its effectiveness in text retrieval based on keyword matching, semantic similarity, and rank fusion. In addition, we provide insights into query latency and the trade-off between efficiency and effectiveness. scrydb is available under the MIT license.

</details>


### [151] [AdaWidth: Query-Adaptive Embedding Width for Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23862)
*Shubing Yang,Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出AdaWidth方法，通过共享前缀表示和轻量级路由器，根据每个查询动态调整评估维度数，在保持检索性能的同时显著减少计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高维嵌入降维方法（如固定截断或静态掩码）无法适应单个任务中不同查询对稳定排名所需维度的巨大差异，导致资源浪费或性能损失。

Method: 引入AdaWidth：1) 使用正交前缀适配器对查询和文档应用单一学习旋转，将判别信号集中在前导坐标；2) 设计轻量级路由器，基于已产生的排名顺序统计信息，仅对可能改变顶级结果的查询评估更多维度。

Result: 在六个检索任务和五个冻结编码器上，AdaWidth在NDCG@10指标上与最先进的降维方法相当，但每个查询使用的维度数减少了55%至84%。理论分析表明，所需维度数随语料库大小对数增长，随检索深度对数下降，且跨查询呈重尾分布。

Conclusion: AdaWidth通过自适应维度选择机制，有效平衡了检索精度与计算效率，证明了动态调整维度优于静态降维策略。

Abstract: High-dimensional embeddings are central to dense retrieval, but not all of these dimensions need to be evaluated at retrieval time. Existing methods reduce dimensions in two ways: truncating the same leading dimensions for every query, or masking a different subset for each query while still storing and accessing the full embedding. Yet queries within a single task differ widely in the number of dimensions they need for their rankings to stabilize.
  We introduce AdaWidth, which adapts the number of evaluated dimensions to each query within a shared prefix representation. An orthogonal prefix adapter applies a single learned rotation to queries and documents alike, concentrating discriminative signal in leading coordinates while leaving every full width inner product unchanged. A lightweight router then reads order statistics off the ranking a query has already produced, and evaluates more dimensions only for the queries whose top results would change. We further derive a prefix sufficiency analysis showing that the required number of dimensions is set by the competing documents at the retrieval cutoff: it grows logarithmically with corpus size, decreases logarithmically with retrieval depth, and remains heavy-tailed across queries. Across six retrieval tasks and five frozen encoders, AdaWidth matches the NDCG@10 of state-of-the-art dimensionality reduction using 55% to 84% fewer dimensions per query.

</details>


### [152] [Wontopos Tablet 2: Measuring Multilingual and Multimodal Memory Retrieval Without Lexical Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23920)
*Sunwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 评估了语言模型长期记忆引擎tablet-2在文本基准和跨语言图像检索上的表现，指出其检索路径不含词汇匹配或独立语言模型。结果显示其在LongMemEval-S上得分95.7%，在BEAM-1M上为67.5%。论文强调这些分数受读者、重问预算等设置影响较大，且对比传统方法（如BM25）和多模态基线时，揭示了现有方法在跨语言和无文本图像检索中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在评估tablet-2作为语言模型生产级长期记忆引擎的性能，特别是在无文本跨语言图像检索场景下的能力，并澄清单纯依赖基准分数无法全面反映系统真实效能，因为细微的设置变化会导致显著结果波动。

Method: 使用两个主要文本基准（LongMemEval-S和BEAM-1M）以及跨语言图像检索任务进行测试。控制变量包括引擎、语料库、设置和评判者，仅改变读者或重问预算以观察敏感度。同时与BM25及Open dense baselines进行对比，测试不同语言（英语、俄语、泰卢固语、斯瓦希里语等）下的表现。

Result: 1. 文本基准：LongMemEval-S得分为95.7% [93.4, 97.1]；BEAM-1M得分为67.5% [64.8, 70.2]。
2. 敏感性分析：仅改变读者使LongMemEval-S得分变化2.0点；仅改变重问预算使BEAM-1M得分变化8.9点。
3. 图像检索：相比BM25（平均recall@5为19.0%），tablet-2达到95.2%；BM25在54个案例中完全为零。
4. 多模态基线对比：Open dense baselines在英语上得91.0%，但在俄语上仅4.7%，且在泰卢固语和斯瓦希里语上崩溃。tablet-2在多语言间的性能波动（14.0）小于基线（27.5/27.7）。
5. 负面结果：低资源语言（斯瓦希里语53.0%，泰卢固语64.0%）表现下降；添加标题使跨语言检索降低11.4点；特定配置遗漏导致韩语top-1准确率损失37点。

Conclusion: tablet-2在无文本跨语言检索方面优于传统词汇匹配方法（如BM25）和部分多模态基线，展现出更强的语言独立性。然而，基准分数对评估设置高度敏感，且系统在低资源语言和特定配置下仍存在显著性能瓶颈。因此，报告结果应视为位置参考而非绝对排名，需结合具体设置解读。

Abstract: We measure tablet-2, a production long-term memory engine for language models, on the text benchmarks the field already uses and on cross-lingual retrieval of photographs stored with no text at all. Its retrieval path contains no lexical matching, no keyword scoring, and no language model of its own.
  On LongMemEval-S (500 questions) it scores 95.7% [93.4, 97.1]; on BEAM-1M (700 questions, 2.21M stored memories) 67.5% [64.8, 70.2]. Those are question-sampling intervals, not the run-to-run spread, which is an order of magnitude narrower.
  Most of the paper is about how little they mean alone. Holding engine, corpus, settings and judge fixed, changing only the reader moves LongMemEval-S by 2.0 points; changing only the re-ask budget moves BEAM-1M by 8.9. Neither is stated in the reports we compare against, and the second exceeds most gaps there, so we give that table as a placement and not a ranking.
  For the multimodal axis we run two controls. Against BM25, configured as strongly as we could, we reach 95.2% mean recall@5 over 70 store-and-query language cells where BM25 reaches 19.0% and is exactly zero in 54. On captionless photographs a lexical method has no document to score at all. Open dense baselines on 300 Crossmodal-3600 photographs in 14 languages show that density confers no language independence: one scores 91.0% on English and 4.7% on Russian from identical image vectors, and a multilingual variant collapses on Telugu and Swahili. Our spread across languages is 14.0 against their 27.5 and 27.7.
  Three results run against us and are reported at equal weight: low-resource languages degrade sharply (Swahili 53.0%, Telugu 64.0%), attaching captions lowers cross-lingual retrieval by 11.4 points, and one setting omitted into one stage of our own retrieval cost 37 points of Korean top-1 accuracy while leaving nine languages untouched.

</details>


### [153] [Hybrid Semantic Tool Discovery for Enterprise MCP Gateway: Architecture and Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23992)
*Olympia Saha,Amy Wang,Srinivasan Manoharan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SCOUT通过引入两个MCP元工具（tool_search和execute_tool），将工具暴露重构为上下文选择问题。它利用混合检索（BM25+向量搜索）动态筛选相关工具，解决了LLM代理在大规模工具库中面临的上下文窗口饱和和工具发现困难问题。在PayPal的生产环境中，该方案将工具Token消耗降低99%（从70.1%降至0.8%），显著降低了推理成本，且具备模型无关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Model Context Protocol (MCP) 架构下两大挑战：1. 上下文工程瓶颈：完整工具模式占满模型上下文窗口，导致在用户查询前即耗尽容量；2. 工具可发现性障碍：用户在200+服务器、2000+工具中难以定位最佳工具。现有提示缓存技术无法释放上下文容量或提高准确性。

Method: 提出SCOUT（Selective Context Optimization for Universal Tooling）框架，核心机制包括：1. 将工具暴露转化为上下文选择问题，仅注入当前步骤相关的工具；2. 定义两个标准MCP元工具：tool_search用于混合检索（融合BM25稀疏匹配与密集向量搜索，通过RRF算法返回Top-k相关工具），execute_tool用于执行；3. 支持零停机目录更新流水线。

Result: 在PayPal生产环境部署中，SCOUT将MCP工具Token消耗从140.2k tokens（占上下文70.1%）大幅降低至1.3k tokens（占0.8%），实现99%的削减，有效降低了企业级规模的单次查询推理成本。

Conclusion: SCOUT成功解决了大规模工具生态中的上下文饱和与发现难题，因其作为标准MCP工具暴露，具有模型无关性且无需客户端修改，具备高通用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents invoke external tools to retrieve and reason over information beyond pretrained knowledge. The Model Context Protocol (MCP) standardizes how such tools are surfaced, and a proxy MCP server aggregates many backend servers behind a single endpoint providing a secure, governable chokepoint for authentication, policy enforcement, and observability. This architecture creates two compounding challenges: a context-engineering bottleneck where full tool schemas saturate the model context window before any user query, and a tool discoverability barrier where users and agents cannot identify the best tool among 2,000+ indexed tools across 200+ MCP servers. Prompt caching reduces reprocessing cost but neither frees context capacity nor improves accuracy. We present SCOUT (Selective Context Optimization for Universal Tooling), which reframes tool exposure as a context-selection problem, injecting only tools relevant to the current step. SCOUT surfaces two MCP meta-tools -- tool_search and execute_tool -- where tool_search performs hybrid retrieval, fusing BM25 sparse matching with dense vector search via Reciprocal Rank Fusion to return the top-k relevant tools. Backed by zero-downtime catalog update pipelines, SCOUT resolves both context saturation and tool discovery challenges. In production at PayPal, SCOUT reduces MCP tool-token consumption from 140.2k tokens (70.1% of context) to 1.3k tokens (0.8%), a 99% reduction, cutting per-query inference cost at enterprise scale. Because SCOUT is surfaced as standard MCP tools, it is model-agnostic and requires no client-side modifications.

</details>


### [154] [TAGR: Temporally Adaptive Generative Recommendation for Industrial Live-Streaming Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24034)
*Wencai Ye,Guangyi Liu,Chaoyi Wang,Wenbin Luo,Shengyu Wang,Mingjie Sun,Peng Wang,Quanming Yao,Wenjin Wu,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TAGR框架，通过时间自适应解决直播广告推荐中的静态ID、单一行为建模和偏好优化冲突问题，显著提升点击率和收入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐模型在静态领域设计，无法适应直播广告中快速变化的内容、产品和用户反馈，导致无法追踪动态广告、捕捉转移意图以及平衡新鲜反馈与训练稳定性。

Method: 提出TAGR框架，包含三个层面的时间自适应机制：1) 使用LSID定期刷新活跃广告的语义ID以适配当前场景；2) 利用IAG在多时间粒度上建模进入历史作为主要意图序列，并结合业务价值加权预测；3) 采用IOPO策略，定期从当前策略采样并进行行为与价值对齐的偏好更新，同时保持监督学习以稳定分布。

Result: 在大规模电商直播广告平台上部署，相比生产基线，直播间进入率提升8.5%，购物车点击率提升7.4%，收入提升16.1%。

Conclusion: 证明了时间自适应生成式推荐在直播广告场景下的有效性和工业可行性。

Abstract: Live-streaming advertising is an important monetization channel on short-video and e-commerce platforms, where rapidly changing live content, promoted products, and user feedback impose strong freshness requirements on recommendation models. Existing generative recommenders designed for static domains fail at three levels: static semantic IDs (SID) cannot track evolving live ads; single-scale behavior modeling misses shifting intent; preference optimization conflicts between fresh on-policy feedback and training stability. We propose TAGR, a generative recommendation framework with temporal adaptation at three levels: live-ad tokenization, user intent modeling, and preference alignment. At the token level, Live Semantic-Collaborative ID (LSID) periodically refreshes each active ad's SID based on its current live scene and promoted products, while retaining a stable hierarchical token vocabulary for autoregressive generation. At the intent level, Intent-Aware Generation (IAG) models live-room entry histories at multiple temporal granularities as the primary intent sequence, keeps auxiliary behaviors as separate inputs, and weights next-token prediction (NTP) using post-request intent evidence and business value. At the alignment level, Intermittent On-Policy Preference Optimization (IOPO) periodically samples fresh candidate groups from the current policy and performs behavior- and value-aligned preference updates interleaved with supervised NTP maintenance to preserve learned behavior distribution. Deployed on a large-scale e-commerce live-stream advertising platform, TAGR improves live-room entry and shopping-cart click rates by 8.5% and 7.4%, respectively, and achieves a 16.1% revenue lift over the production baseline. These results demonstrate the effectiveness and industrial viability of temporally adaptive generative recommendation for live-stream advertising.

</details>


### [155] [RetrievalFormer: A Dual-Encoder Transformer for Efficient Approximate Nearest Neighbor Retrieval and Cold-Item Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24079)
*Theodore Rogers,Joe Standerfer,Dmitrii Timoshenko,Haoxue Li,Zuhaib Akhtar,Soyoung Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究对比了基于特征的共享搜索-推荐索引与需重训练的ID-softmax推荐器。发现特征塔在冷启动和开放新物品方面表现优异，但在热启动精度上略逊于精心调优的重训练基线；精确全Softmax训练能显著提升性能但受限于内存成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决共享搜索与推荐索引中必须仅凭特征对新物品进行评分的问题，因为搜索场景没有探索槽位。旨在平衡推荐系统的开放性（支持新物品）与准确性，并评估不同训练策略（采样负样本、全Softmax、近似搜索）的效果及局限。

Method: 1. 使用双编码器检索架构保持索引对新品开放。2. 在MovieLens-1M和MIND数据集上，将基于内容的塔（Content Tower）与六个经过五轮重训练和调优的序列基线模型进行对比。3. 修复了导致19.7%用户目标排序错误的float32时间戳Bug。4. 评估了在严格零泄漏冷启动设置下的表现，以及精确全Softmax训练（InfoNCE采样 vs 全目录计算）的性能差异和内存消耗。5. 分析近似最近邻搜索（ANN）和历史窗口扫描对剩余性能差距的解释程度。

Result: 1. **开放性优势**：特征塔能有效服务从未展示过的新物品（38.6%的查询印象为全新），且对用户冷启动交互无显著损失（Recall@20: 0.9595 vs 0.9510）。2. **热启动劣势**：在MovieLens-1M上，其准确率比最强重训练基线低5.2% (Recall@20) 和11.4% (NDCG@20)；在MIND上差距缩小至0.8-3.6%，但仍排名第六/七。3. **冷启动优势**：在无冷启动特定训练的情况下，内容塔的Recall@20为0.172，是强重训练方法(0.124)的1.4倍，是无训练基准的3倍。4. **精确训练代价**：精确全Softmax训练在MIND-small和MovieLens-1M上分别提升Recall@20达54%和6.9%，但在24万物品规模下耗尽加速器内存。5. **其他因素**：ANN无法解释剩余差距，历史窗口扫描解释了约一半的残差。

Conclusion: 双编码器检索通过保持索引开放来适应新物品和冷启动需求，虽在热启动精度上略低于重度优化的重训练模型，但在冷启动场景中具有显著优势。精确全Softmax训练能带来巨大性能提升，但面临巨大的计算和内存挑战，因此在大规模目录下的精确质量训练仍是一个未解决的开放性问题。

Abstract: A shared search-and-recommendation index must score new items from features alone because search has no exploration slot. In a public log covering both surfaces over one catalog, $38.6\%$ of held-out query-search impressions show an item never previously shown or visited. For user-cold engagements, the feature-based tower serves this demand without measurable loss against $99$ sampled negatives ($0.9595$ Recall@20 versus $0.9510$ warm). A lexical baseline reaches similar parity, while a full-catalog check remains statistically undecided. Dual-encoder retrieval therefore keeps the index \emph{open} to new items, unlike an ID-softmax recommender that requires retraining. We price this openness on recommendation against six sequential baselines, each retrained and tuned through five rounds on corrected targets. A float32 timestamp bug had reordered leave-one-out targets for $19.7\%$ of users. On MovieLens-1M, warm accuracy trails the strongest retrained baseline by $5.2\%$ Recall@20 and $11.4\%$ NDCG@20. On MIND, the gap narrows to $0.8$--$3.6\%$ relative to the five strongest baselines, though the model ranks sixth of seven. Under strict zero-leakage cold-start evaluation, the content tower achieves $0.172 \pm 0.006$ Recall@20, $1.4\times$ the strongest retrained dedicated method ($0.124 \pm 0.007$) and $3\times$ a training-free floor, without cold-specific training. Exact full-softmax training raises Recall@20 by $54\%$ on MIND-small and $6.9\%$ on MovieLens-1M over sampled InfoNCE, but recomputes the full catalog each step and exhausts accelerator memory at $240$K items. Approximate nearest-neighbor search explains none of the remaining gap, serving cost does not regress against ID-softmax retrieval, and a history-window sweep explains half the post-recipe remainder. Exact-quality training at catalog scale remains the open problem.

</details>


### [156] [CodeHID: Learning an Addressable Hierarchical Code Index for Generative Code Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24089)
*Zhen Li,Yuhong Chen,Wenhao Xu,Xiaodong Li,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CodeHID框架，通过生成式检索将代码匹配转化为层次化语义地址生成，解决传统扁平匹配区分度低及直接应用生成式检索语义空间无序的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码检索依赖扁平匹配范式，难以区分相似代码；直接应用生成式检索可能导致标识符前缀缺乏有意义的语义对应关系。

Method: 提出CodeHID框架，包含两个核心组件：1. 伪邻居引导的DocID学习：利用多级残差量化和k-NN伪监督构建全局静态层次索引，确保语义相关代码共享前缀且保持可分性；2. 双阶段DocID生成指导：结合训练端排名增强（难负样本、排名蒸馏）与推理端候选约束及前缀感知解码。

Result: 在CoSQA和ProCQA基准测试中，CodeHID在大多数情况下显著优于稀疏检索、预训练代码模型、密集代码检索及生成式检索基线，尤其在Rank-one检索指标上提升明显。

Conclusion: CodeHID通过将任务重构为粗到细的语义地址生成，有效提升了代码检索的性能和准确性。

Abstract: Code retrieval models have predominantly relied on a flat matching paradigm that treats code snippets as independent candidates, making them less capable of distinguishing similar code candidates. Generative retrieval offers a solution by constructing a learnable index over the code corpus, guiding the retriever to better understand how code candidates are semantically organized and addressed. However, naively applying generative retrieval in the code retrieval task may result in operating over an identifier space whose prefixes do not correspond to meaningful code-semantic regions. In this paper, we propose CodeHID, a generative code retrieval framework that reformulates the code retrieval task from flat candidate matching to coarse-to-fine semantic address generation. CodeHID relies on two core components. First, Pseudo-Neighbor Guided DocID Learning constructs a globally static hierarchical index by applying multi-level residual quantization and $k$-nearest-neighbor pseudo-supervision, ensuring that semantically related code snippets share prefixes while preserving target-level separability. Second, Dual-Phase DocID Generation Guidance reliably navigates this fixed index by combining training-side ranking enhancements, using hard negatives and rank distillation, with inference-side candidate constraints and prefix-aware decoding. Extensive experiments on CoSQA and ProCQA benchmarks demonstrate that CodeHID outperforms existing sparse retrieval, pre-trained code models, dense code retrieval, and generative retrieval baselines by a large margin in most cases, achieving particularly strong improvements in rank-one retrieval metrics.

</details>


### [157] [Native Multimodal Representation Learning for Click-Through Rate Prediction in E-Commerce Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24091)
*Chao Yi,Feifan Yang,Jiawei Feng,Sishuo Chen,Zhangming Chan,Xiang-Rong Sheng,Han Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出“先挖掘后训练”（Mine-Then-Train）方法，通过从CTR数据中筛选高质量、多模态可解释样本微调编码器，解决端到端训练中因监督信号模糊导致的性能瓶颈，提升推荐系统效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两阶段范式下，多模态预训练目标与CTR预测任务存在分布差异；尝试端到端联合训练虽直观但无效，因用户行为受非多模态因素干扰导致监督信号模糊及编码器更新不一致。

Method: 提出“先挖掘后训练”策略：首先从原始CTR数据中挖掘高质量且多模态可解释的训练样本，随后利用这些样本对多模态编码器进行微调，使其更贴合用户点击偏好。

Result: 离线和在线实验均证明该方法的有效性，成功解决了端到端训练带来的性能停滞问题，提升了下游CTR预测任务的准确率。

Conclusion: 直接端到端训练并非解决多模态表示与下游任务对齐的最佳途径；通过精选高质量样本进行针对性微调，能显著优化多模态表示在工业级电商推荐系统中的表现。

Abstract: Multimodal representations have been widely adopted in industrial e-commerce recommendation systems. Due to their strong semantic understanding and generalization capabilities, they enhance the performance of traditional sparse ID-based Click-Through Rate (CTR) prediction models. Current multimodal application frameworks in the CTR prediction task typically follow a two-stage paradigm: first, pre-training a multimodal encoder on data from specific recommendation scenarios; second, extracting items' multimodal representations using this pre-trained multimodal encoder and integrating them into the CTR prediction model. However, the training objectives and data distribution of multimodal pre-training tasks often differ from those of the CTR prediction task, which limits the effectiveness of multimodal representation on downstream tasks. In this paper, we focus on how to learn Native Multimodal Representation for the CTR prediction task. One intuitive solution is to jointly train the multimodal encoder and CTR model end-to-end on the CTR task, with the expectation that the encoder can automatically learn downstream-relevant knowledge. However, we find that the end-to-end training does not bring performance improvements to existing multimodal application paradigms. Our analysis reveals that user behaviors in raw CTR data are driven by both multimodal semantics and non-multimodal factors, leading to ambiguous supervision and inconsistent encoder updates. To address this, we propose a Mine-Then-Train method that mines high-quality, multimodally interpretable training samples from CTR data and uses them to fine-tune the multimodal encoder for better alignment with user click preferences. Offline and online experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [158] [Tlow: Flow-based Item Tokenizer for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24176)
*Nian Li,Chonggang Song,Jingtao Ding,Lingling Yi,Yong Li,Qingmin Liao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出基于流的方法 Tlow 作为 Item Tokenizer，通过分布变换解决传统 RQ-VAE 解码效率低及 OPQ 维度相关性高的问题，显著提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐模型中随机物品 ID 导致参数冗余和冷启动问题；现有主流 Tokenizer（如 RQ-VAE）因码本依赖导致解码效率低，而高效独立 Tokenizer（如 OPQ）难以处理语义嵌入的维度相关性和分布复杂性。

Method: 1. 提出基于流的方法 Tlow：将原始语义嵌入转换为潜在空间，使其符合统一的标准正态分布，实现维度独立和分布简化。2. 引入代码本引导机制：对齐代码本空间与令牌嵌入空间，促进学习更具语义区分度的令牌嵌入。3. 在简化嵌入空间上进行独立令牌化以生成语义清晰的 Token IDs。

Result: 1. 离线实验：在四个公开数据集上显著提升了推荐性能。2. 跨领域/多模态实验：验证了简化嵌入空间中物品令牌化的有效性。3. 在线实验：在微信大规模多模态检索任务中，基于 Token IDs 的检索模型全局 CTR 提升 10.32%，新物品 CTR 提升 11.64%。

Conclusion: Tlow 通过强大的分布变换能力和代码本引导，有效解决了语义嵌入处理的复杂性问题，证明了简化嵌入空间在推荐系统中的巨大潜力和实际价值。

Abstract: Item tokenizer encodes semantic embeddings into token IDs to replace the randomly assigned item IDs used in traditional recommendation models, fundamentally addressing the problems of excessive parameters and cold starts. However, the most common tokenizer, RQ-VAE, suffers from low decoding efficiency due to the inherent dependencies among its codebooks. Meanwhile, efficient independent tokenizers such as optimized product quantization (OPQ) still struggle with dimensional correlations and distribution complexity of semantic embeddings. In this work, we propose a f\underline{low}-based item \underline{T}okenizer (Tlow) to transform raw semantic embeddings into a latent space where embeddings conform to a unified standard normal distribution, achieving dual advantages of dimensional independence and distributional simplicity. Independent tokenization performed on these latent embeddings yields semantically clear token IDs. Additionally, we introduce a novel codebook guidance to align the codebook space with the token embedding space, further aiding the learning of more semantically distinct token embeddings. Offline experiments on four public datasets demonstrate that Tlow's tokenization and codebook guidance significantly improve recommendation performance. The improvement on cross-domain and multi-modal recommendations also proves the effectiveness of item tokenization in a simplified embedding space. Online experiments for a multi-modal retrieval task on China's largest social media platform WeChat validate Tlow's powerful distribution transformation capability. The retrieval model based on token IDs improves user CTR by 10.32\% globally and by 11.64\% for new items. Our codes are available at https://github.com/wjjln/Tlow.

</details>


### [159] [RecGPT-Mobile-V2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24295)
*Lingqing Zhang,Bin Zhang,Weipeng Huang,Chengfei Lv,Chengyu Lai,Chuxin Chen,Dimin Wang,Han Zhu,Hongtao Cheng,Jialin Zhu,Jian Wang,Jiuning Lin,Junqing Wu,Li Chen,Qichao Ma,Ruiquan Lan,Shuai Zhong,Tao Wang,Xiaodong Zhu,Yinjiang Cai,Yinnan Song,Yipeng Yu,Yuan Liu,Yuning Jiang,Zhaode Wang,Zhibo Xiao,Zhixin Ma,Zihong Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出RecGPT-Mobile-V2框架，通过证据保留轨迹、领域适应及推理成本优化，实现端侧个性化查询预测的意图质量与执行效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决端侧部署中行为数据噪声大、多尺度、多解并存的问题，避免统一推理策略导致的计算浪费或能力不足。

Method: 构建端到端框架：将异构交互转化为证据保留轨迹；通过领域适应和 supervised alignment 建立推荐原生基础模型；仅在满足 grounding 和 utility 标准后应用推理成本优化；最终蒸馏为低比特执行、结构化压缩及预算感知路由的学生模型。

Result: 证据聚焦的短思维链提升ROUGE-L至0.315、Jaccard至0.248；完整奖励机制使查询质量提升至78.6%，硬失败率降至1.6%，中位思维链长度从62降至14 token；在线分析显示其检索库存与现有通道互补。

Conclusion: 支持“充分性导向”而非均匀简短的推理策略：保留决策相关证据，仅在可能提升预测质量时分配额外计算资源。

Abstract: Personalized Query prediction maps implicit behavioral signals---clicks, favorites, purchases, and post-purchase exploration---to explicit retrieval intent. On-device deployment makes this task particularly challenging: behavioral trajectories are noisy and multi-scale, multiple Queries may be valid for a single trajectory, and a uniform reasoning policy either expends unnecessary computation on simple instances or allocates insufficient capacity to complex ones. We introduce RecGPT-Mobile-V2, an end-to-end framework that treats intent quality and execution efficiency as coupled objectives within a staged design. The framework transforms heterogeneous interactions into an evidence-preserving trajectory, establishes a recommendation-native foundation through domain adaptation and supervised alignment, and applies reasoning-cost optimization only after grouped rollouts meet grounding and utility criteria. The resulting teacher is distilled into a compact student deployed with low-bit execution, structured compression, and budget-aware device--cloud routing. In an aligned CoT ablation, an evidence-focused short rationale increases ROUGE-L from 0.228 to 0.315 and Jaccard from 0.174 to 0.248, while slightly outperforming the full five-stage rationale. In the controlled RL comparison, the complete reward formulation improves Query quality from 73.2% under quality-only RL to 78.6%, lowers the hard-failure rate from 3.6% to 1.6%, and reduces the median CoT length from 62 to 14 tokens. Online retrieval analysis further indicates that the Query recall channel retrieves inventory complementary to that surfaced by established recall channels. Collectively, these findings support sufficiency-oriented rather than uniformly short reasoning: retain decision-relevant evidence and allocate additional computation only when it is likely to improve the predicted Query.

</details>


### [160] [Rethinking Semantic Alignment in LLM-Enhanced Collaborative Filtering: A Spectral Decoupling Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24363)
*Yedong Jin,Shaowen Peng,Tsunenori Mine,Shoko Wakamiya,Eiji Aramaki*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文从谱分析角度重新审视LLM增强推荐，发现协同信号与语义信号在频谱上互补。现有对齐方法导致非主语义成分丢失，提出UniSpecRec通过谱滤波解耦并融合预测，无需跨空间对齐即可提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM增强推荐方法通常将语义表示与协同嵌入在共享空间中对其，但这种方式如何影响LLM编码的信息尚不清楚，且可能未能充分利用互补的语义信息。

Method: 提出UniSpecRec（统一推荐中的谱信号），采用谱特定滤波技术，保持协同和语义表示在各自空间中，通过组合两者的预测结果实现融合，无需跨空间对齐或额外可训练参数。

Result: 实验表明，非主奇异值分量在对齐下收益不一致，但在组件级解耦下持续改善性能；全预测级解耦效果最佳。UniSpecRec在有效性、效率和泛化性上均表现优异。

Conclusion: 对齐机制无法有效利用互补的非主语义信息，通过谱域解耦而非空间对齐能更有效地结合协同与语义信号，提升推荐性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM-enhanced recommendation commonly align semantic representations with collaborative embeddings in a shared space, yet how alignment affects LLM-encoded information remains unclear. In this work, we revisit LLM-enhanced recommendation from a spectral perspective and show that collaborative and semantic signals benefit from different spectral parts. While collaborative representations are dominated by smooth low-frequency components due to user-item homophily, semantic embeddings contain useful non-principal singular components. Through component-wise evaluation and training-dynamics analysis, we find that alignment increasingly concentrates learned representations in dominant collaborative and principal semantic subspaces, reducing overlap with non-principal semantic components. Controlled comparisons show that non-principal components provide inconsistent gains under alignment but consistently improve performance through component-level decoupling, while full prediction-level decoupling achieves the best overall performance. These results indicate that alignment fails to effectively exploit complementary non-principal semantic information. Motivated by these findings, we propose UniSpecRec (Unifying Spectral Signals for Recommendation), which applies signal-specific spectral filtering while preserving collaborative and semantic representations in their respective spaces. UniSpecRec combines their predictions without cross-space alignment or additional trainable parameters. Extensive experiments demonstrate its effectiveness, efficiency, and generalizability.

</details>


### [161] [EviGraph: Towards Verifiable Evidence Construction for Information-Seeking Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24667)
*Jiashun Chen,Yirong Mao,Wenhui Que*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: EviGraph通过分离搜索执行与证据记录，利用强化学习直接监督证据构建过程，显著提升了Agentic Web搜索的准确性及效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体在检索信息时，往往无法确保证据支持最终答案中的主张；且传统方法采用线性交互轨迹，仅优化最终答案正确性，对中间过程的 grounding（接地/依据）缺乏有效监督。

Method: 提出EviGraph深度搜索框架：1) 将搜索执行与证据记录分离，但使用共享策略处理可训练角色；2) Executor规划简洁查询；3) 冻结的证据验证器检查源页面并返回带明确极性（polarity）的字面证据项；4) 策略将这些证据项映射为add/support图请求，并由确定性结构验证器检查；5) 生成的图作为持久工作记忆和密集过程奖励来源，用于强化学习以直接监督证据构建。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus基准上，Qwen3-8B EviGraph智能体在相同交互预算下准确率达35.9%，显著优于无强化学习的同架构双角色模型（26.9%）和单体智能体（2.7%），且每轮 rollout 生成更少的 token。在BrowseComp、GAIA和XBench上也获得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 显式地结构化证据记录并给予奖励，能够有效改进 Agentic Web 搜索的性能。

Abstract: Agentic Web search can retrieve relevant information without establishing that the retrieved content actually supports the claims used in an answer. Existing agents typically keep search and evidence recording in a linear interaction trace and optimize primarily for final-answer correctness, providing limited supervision for intermediate grounding. We present EviGraph, a deep-search framework that separates search execution from evidence recording while using a shared policy for the trainable roles. An executor plans concise queries, a frozen evidence verifier inspects source pages and returns verbatim evidence items with an explicit polarity, and the policy maps those items to add/support graph requests that are checked by a deterministic structural validator. The resulting graph serves both as persistent working memory and as a source of dense process rewards, enabling reinforcement learning to directly supervise evidence construction rather than only the final answer. On BrowseComp-Plus, a Qwen3-8B EviGraph agent achieves 35.9% accuracy under a matched interaction budget, compared with 26.9% for the same dual-role architecture without reinforcement learning and 2.7% for a monolithic agent, while generating fewer tokens per rollout. Consistent gains on BrowseComp, GAIA, and XBench indicate that explicitly structuring and rewarding evidence recording improves agentic search

</details>


### [162] [Auditing Return Conditioning as a Control Knob: An Offline Diagnostic for Decision Transformer Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24815)
*Jingyu Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究通过离线设置对比了不同干预范围（全上下文 vs. 仅当前token）对推荐模型控制性的影响，发现MovieLens数据集上全上下文干预显著改变预测分布，而MyAnimeList上无此效果，表明现有的离线RTG审计方法未能确立可靠的奖励控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在测试基于返回路径（Return-to-Go, RTG）条件的推荐模型的可控性，并解决目前很少对这种干预进行审计的问题，特别是区分重写历史RTG token与仅重写当前token所产生的不同局部性效应。

Method: 在MovieLens 25M和MyAnimeList 2020数据集上，使用决策变压器（Decision Transformer），采用固定窗口进行离线评估。主要实验包括：RTG局部性阶梯测试、无RTG基线对照、日志匹配与评分奖励检查，以及轨迹内打乱RTG的消融实验。

Result: 1. MovieLens数据集中，覆盖全上下文的干预（K=20）使犯罪类型预测占比显著增加（+23.61%），而仅更改当前槽位的影响较小（+1.77%）。2. 打乱的RTG模型几乎消除了这种响应。3. MyAnimeList数据集未观察到类似的戏剧类型响应。4. 各类别下的 genre 预测准确率在不同RTG配置下数值接近，且K=1时的日志匹配率变化不大。

Conclusion: 跨局部性、打乱RTG以及MyAnimeList上的零结果所呈现的模式，并未确立有效的奖励控制。研究提出了四项检查标准以改进审计：干预局部性、无RTG基线、奖励检查和RTG内容消融。

Abstract: Offline return-to-go (RTG) sweeps can test whether a recommender conditioned on return is controllable, but the intervention is rarely audited. Rewriting every historical RTG token creates an increasingly synthetic context, while rewriting only the current token is more local. We test this distinction in an offline setting with a fixed window. On MovieLens 25M and MyAnimeList 2020 (MAL), we evaluate a Decision Transformer using an RTG locality ladder, a control without RTG, a logged match and score reward check, and a within-trajectory shuffled RTG ablation. On MovieLens, a $K=20$ intervention that covers the full context, applied only to real context positions, shifts the share of Crime predictions by $+23.61 \pm 2.96$ percentage points from the validation 5th to 95th percentile, whereas changing only the current slot shifts it by $+1.77 \pm 1.17$ points. The shuffled RTG model largely removes this response ($+2.08 \pm 1.20$ points at $K=20$). On MAL, the same protocol does not produce a Drama response: $K=20$ changes Drama by $-0.03 \pm 0.07$ points, and $K=1$ by $-0.01 \pm 0.01$. Genre prediction accuracy is numerically close across real RTG, no RTG, and shuffled RTG, and at $K=1$ logged match rates and matched ratings change little. Because dataset and focus-genre selection were exploratory, these magnitudes are descriptive; the cross-diagnostic pattern across locality, shuffled RTG, and the null result on MAL does not establish reward control. We propose four checks: intervention locality, a no-RTG baseline, a reward check, and RTG-content ablation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [Matched Excess-Outranker Regularization for Candidate-Set Interference in Continual Knowledge Graph Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24273)
*Hao Ren,Junbin Gao,Jiaojiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MEOR正则化方法，解决持续知识图谱嵌入中新实体加入导致的“候选集干扰”问题，显著提升历史查询的排名稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习主要关注灾难性遗忘，但新实体的加入会改变兼容查询的候选集范围，导致即使旧实体的得分和相对顺序未变，其绝对排名也可能因新竞争者而下降（即候选集干扰）。

Method: 引入匹配超额排他正则化（MEOR），这是一种主机级目标函数。它通过比较平滑的新来者压力与结构匹配的旧参考对象，仅在新生竞争超过匹配参考时施加单向惩罚，从而保留对合法新实体的学习信号。

Result: 在ENTITY-ComplEx数据集上，相比重放方法，MEOR将历史当前宇宙平均倒数排名（MRR）提升0.0057，并将候选集干扰降低0.0055；在FBInc-S/L设置中均显著改善历史排名，且统计显著性得到验证。

Conclusion: 证实了实体准入是持续排名损失的一个独立来源，并表明在不替换底层嵌入架构或持续学习器的情况下，可以通过控制候选集干扰来维持历史排名性能。

Abstract: Continual knowledge graph embedding updates entity and relation representations as a graph grows. Existing methods primarily address catastrophic forgetting, but entity admission also changes the candidate universe of every compatible query. A historical answer can therefore lose rank even when its score and its ordering among old entities are preserved. We formalize this effect as candidate-set interference and introduce Matched Excess-Outranker Regularization (MEOR), a host-level objective that compares smooth answer-relative newcomer pressure with score-blind, structurally matched old references. Its one-sided penalty acts only when newcomer competition exceeds the matched reference, preserving the host learner's signal for legitimate new entities. Across eight paired runs on ENTITY-ComplEx, MEOR improves historical current-universe mean reciprocal rank (MRR) by 0.0057 over replay and reduces candidate-set interference by 0.0055, with one-sided 95% lower bounds of 0.0052 and 0.0051, respectively. It satisfies the preservation criteria for old-universe ranking and newcomer acquisition and improves historical current-universe MRR over persistent calibration, matched maximum regularizer (MMR), and unmatched old regularizer (UOR). Direct ablations support each component of its reference construction and aggregation. Adding MEOR also improves historical ranking in all ten reported FBInc-S and FBInc-L host and backbone settings, with every paired 95% confidence interval excluding zero. These results establish candidate admission as a distinct source of continual rank loss and show that it can be controlled without replacing the underlying embedding architecture or continual learner.

</details>


### [164] [RENDER: Controlling Reader-Facing Evidence in LLM Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23568)
*Yuan Si,Simeng Han,Daming Li,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RENDER基准，证明RAG/记忆评估中“读者端呈现形式”显著影响模型表现，建议标准化报告该变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆和RAG评估常将回答模型的输入格式视为实现细节，忽略了不同呈现方式（如摘要、结构化记录、原始对话）对模型性能的潜在巨大影响。

Method: 构建RENDER基准，控制对话内容不变，变化面向读者的输入工件。采用五级数据包层级定位信息进入点，并使用确定性模板模拟ChatGPT风格条目、LangChain摘要、MemGPT结构化记录及原始对话。在500道LongMemEval题目和9个模型上进行测试。

Result: 1. 匹配预算下的解析数据包比截断的原始对话性能高出42.4-72.6分；2. ChatGPT风格条目在7/9模型上优于原始对话；3. 部分模型在结构化账本上得分为0，但在自然语言条目上得分达45.4-53.4%；4. 效果在检索噪声下持续，并迁移至HotpotQA数据集。

Conclusion: 记忆/RAG评估必须报告或控制“读者端工件”这一变量，因为输入呈现形式的差异会导致模型性能产生显著波动。

Abstract: Memory and RAG evaluations often treat the answering model's input as an implementation detail, even though systems may render the same history as a memory entry, summary, typed record, or raw excerpt. We introduce RENDER, a benchmark control that fixes the conversation while varying the reader-facing artifact. RENDER combines a five-level packet ladder, localizing when answer-bearing content enters the input, with deterministic templates approximating ChatGPT-style entries, LangChain summaries, MemGPT-style typed records, and raw conversation. On 500 LongMemEval questions and nine models, matched-budget resolved packets beat recency-truncated raw dialogue by 42.4-72.6 points. In deployed-style templates, best-worst spread is 24.6-48.8 points per model; under the primary scorer, ChatGPT-style entries have higher point estimates than raw conversation on 7 of 9 models. Judge rescoring preserves the positive aggregate effect, but model-specific significance is mixed. Three models scoring 0 percent on formal ledger packets answer the same facts from natural-language entries at 45.4-53.4 percent. The effect persists under retrieval noise and transfers to HotpotQA, suggesting that memory/RAG evaluations should report or control the reader-facing artifact.

</details>


### [165] [ESQ-Bench: A Multi-Tier Enterprise Oracle Benchmark for Evaluating NL2SQL Dialect Generalization and Silent Semantic Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23569)
*Sanjay Mishra,Divya Chukkapalli,Ganesh R. Naik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ESQ-Bench，首个面向企业级Oracle数据库的NL2SQL基准测试，揭示现有模型在复杂企业schema下执行准确率显著下降，且存在严重的“静默分歧”问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的NL2SQL基准（如Spider、BIRD）基于简化的学术schema和开源SQL方言，无法反映企业数据库环境的复杂性，导致模型在实际应用中表现不佳。

Method: 构建包含6个填充schema（465张表，164,682行数据）的ESQ-Bench，覆盖三个复杂度层级；使用Oracle、PostgreSQL、MySQL和SQL Server提供相同种子数据；采用四种评估指标（EM, EX, SR, SD）；通过GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.6和Llama 3.2等模型进行schema-linked提示和零样本测试。

Result: 1. 随着schema复杂度增加，模型执行准确率单调下降（GPT-4o从79.8%降至57.2%）。
2. 准确匹配率（EM）极低（低于7%），而静默分歧率高达73%-99%，表明多数“正确”查询结果语义错误。
3. Claude Sonnet 4.6在各层级表现优于GPT-4o（最高达87.4% EX）。
4. GPT-4o零样本表现因幸存者偏差看似较好，但实际执行率低。
5. 本地开源模型Llama 3.2表现极差（仅13.3% EX），显示闭源API模型与开源基线间的巨大差距。

Conclusion: 企业级NL2SQL任务仍面临严峻挑战，特别是处理复杂schema时的语义正确性和执行稳定性。当前SOTA模型虽在执行匹配上有一定表现，但在结果语义一致性上存在严重缺陷，亟需更贴近真实企业场景的评估方法和更强大的模型能力。

Abstract: State-of-the-art Natural Language to SQL (NL2SQL) models report execution accuracy exceeding 89 percent on established benchmarks such as Spider and BIRD. However, these benchmarks rely on simplified academic schemas and open-source SQL dialects that do not reflect the complexity of enterprise database environments. We introduce ESQ-Bench, an Oracle-first NL2SQL benchmark with systematic complexity tiers and silent-divergence evaluation across three enterprise schema complexity tiers. We constructed and released six populated schemas (465 tables, 164,682 rows, zero empty tables) with identical seed data on Oracle, PostgreSQL, MySQL, and SQL Server, a four-metric evaluation harness (EM, EX, SR, SD), and 550 gold-validated question-query pairs (Tier-1: 95; Tier-2: 228; Tier-3: 227). Schema-linked prompting with GPT-4o shows monotonic execution-match degradation across tiers: 79.8, 60.3, and 57.2 percent EX on executed queries (June 2026), versus 75.6, 80.4, and 95.8 percent on an earlier 142-question pilot slice. EM stays below 7 percent tier-wide; operational silent-divergence reaches 73 to 99 percent among EX-passing queries. Failure analysis shows wrong-result semantics dominate at higher tiers. Claude Sonnet 4.6 with schema-linked prompts reaches 87.4, 74.9, and 68.7 percent EX (executed queries), exceeding GPT-4o schema-linked on every tier. GPT-4o zero-shot EX on executed queries (78.7, 73.5, and 77.8 percent) inverts schema-linked at Tiers 2 to 3 due to lower execution rates and survivor bias in the zero-shot versus schema-linked analysis. Local Llama 3.2 schema-linked reaches only 13.3 percent bank-wide EX (73 out of 550), underscoring the gap between closed API models and open-weight baselines on enterprise Oracle schemas.

</details>


### [166] [LLM Agents Perform Controlled Experiments Using Simulation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23622)
*Yuchen Xia,Michael Weyrich,Nasser Jazdi,Johannes Stümpfle,Johannes Sigel,Akshay Narla,Gavin K. Reynolds,Anna Jawor-Baczynska,Pol Llopart*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种多智能体框架，将大语言模型与高保真仿真模型结合，通过受控实验进行干预推理，优化制药工艺参数，显著提升输出的具体性、正确性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在推理和规划方面表现强劲，但科学工程任务需要理解系统对干预的反应，这依赖于受控实验，而不仅仅是生成合理的文本或代码。

Method: 构建多智能体框架，接收用户查询和基线配置，构建结构化任务表示，设计实验，执行比较仿真，解释结果，并合成基于证据的工艺参数优化建议。

Result: 相比纯语言推理，该系统能产生更具体、可操作的输出；在工业应用中表现出更高的输出具体性、用户评分的正确性和帮助性；消融研究和案例分析验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将语言模型与高保真仿真模型耦合在交互式智能体框架中，支持通过干预、比较和观察进行推理，证明了集成仿真的实验推理在制药工艺设计中的实际效用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in reasoning, planning, and tool use, but many scientific and engineering tasks require more than plausible text and code generation. They require understanding how a system responds to intervention, which in practice depends on controlled experimentation. In this work, we propose a multi-agent framework that enables LLM agents to conduct controlled experiments with scientific simulation models for pharmaceutical process design. Given a user query and a baseline configuration, the system constructs a structured task representation, designs experiments, executes comparative simulation, interprets the resulting outcomes, and synthesizes evidence-based recommendations for process parameter optimization. By coupling language models with high-fidelity simulation models in an interactive agent framework, the proposed system supports reasoning through intervention, comparison, and observation. As a result, it produces more specific and actionable outputs than language-only reasoning. In an industrial application setting, this advantage is reflected in higher output specificity as well as improved user-rated correctness and helpfulness. Ablation studies and visualized case analyses further demonstrate the effectiveness and practical utility of simulation-integrated experimental reasoning.

</details>


### [167] [A survey detection channel overrides the pixels in an astronomical foundation model, and biases tomographic mean redshifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23626)
*Ihor Kendiukhov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现天文学基础模型AION-1的性能严重受限于训练数据目录的不完整性。通过因果干预分析，证实模型存在“检测门控”偏差：其预测结果主要取决于输入掩码中是否存在中心源（而非实际光强），且对错误目录信息的敏感度高于无信息状态。这种由Legacy Survey管线遗漏导致的系统性误差会显著扭曲宇宙学参数（如红移均值），超出LSST DESC要求。此外，模型还受限于标记化器的分辨率不足和量化限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文学基础模型通常使用包含不完整目录的survey像素进行训练，这会导致模型继承系统性偏差。本研究旨在审计AION-1模型，探究其预测机制中的潜在偏差来源及其对宇宙学分析的影响。

Method: 对AION-1（一个39模态Transformer）进行因果干预分析。具体包括：保持图像token不变，仅编辑调查分割图；评估不同输入条件下模型输出（通量、大小、椭圆率、红移）的变化；分析模型对目录信息、位置误差及标记化器限制的敏感性。

Result: 1. 检测门控偏差：仅修改分割图即可使模型报告的所有物理量变化110-4400倍，表明模型依赖字段中心的存在性（r=0.47）而非掩码内的光强（r=0.30）。2. 目录信息负面效应：矛盾的目录光度测量使模型性能比无元数据时差九倍。3. 系统性误差影响：Legacy Survey管线导致3.68%的目标无覆盖，传播该误差会使层析平均红移偏离LSST DESC要求中位数0.71倍，并在12种情况下超标。4. 其他限制：图像编解码器有效状态少（28 vs 934），红读出受量化限制，稀疏字典作为因果手柄不可靠。

Conclusion: AION-1等天文学基础模型的性能受到训练数据目录不完整性的严重影响，特别是“检测门控”机制导致了严重的系统性偏差。这些偏差会显著影响宇宙学参数估计，超出任务要求。移除检测通道可在无显著成本下消除该效应，但效果随模型规模增大而增强。研究强调了改进数据管线、优化标记化器及谨慎处理元数据的重要性。

Abstract: Foundation models for astronomy are trained on survey pixels together with the catalogue products derived from those pixels. Those catalogues are incomplete at a measurable rate, and a model trained on both inherits that incompleteness as a systematic. We audit AION-1, a 39-modality transformer trained on more than 200 million objects, using causal interventions on its inputs.
  Holding the image tokens byte-identical and editing only the survey segmentation map changes every quantity the model reports -- flux, size, ellipticity, redshift -- by 110-4400 times a matched placebo. The mechanism is detection gating, presence at the field centre (r = 0.47), not the light the mask encloses (r = 0.30); across 322 real blends the model ignores how the pipeline partitioned the light (R = -0.006). Nor is the preference specific to that channel: contradicted catalogue photometry leaves the model nine times worse than supplying no metadata at all.
  The Legacy Survey pipeline leaves 3.68% of targets with no segment covering their position. Propagating that rate, with a miss represented by the fields the pipeline actually returns, shifts tomographic mean redshifts by a median 0.71 times the LSST DESC requirement over 40 assignments and exceeds it in 12; observed positional errors take the worst bin to 8.3 times. Drawing the misses by their measured magnitude dependence rather than uniformly does not change it. Spectroscopy removes the effect, withholding the detection channel removes it at no measurable cost, and the effect grows with model scale.
  Two further limits lie in the tokeniser: its image codec resolves 28 effective states on source patches against 934 for the spectrum codec, and the redshift readout is quantisation-limited. Sparse dictionaries are unreliable causal handles: across 15, recovery spans 26-75% and moves up to 18 points on the seed alone.

</details>


### [168] [TRACE: Transition-Aware Residual Control for Multi-Objective Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23631)
*Kang Zhou,Yujia Tong,Yong Tao,Jingling Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TRACE框架，通过记录“父-编辑-子”转换及属性变化来优化多目标材料发现，显著提升了LLM代理的搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理在多目标材料发现中，仅存储候选材料和分数，缺乏对“可执行编辑如何导致有用属性变化”的理解，导致在目标冲突时难以进行有效的局部精炼。

Method: 提出TRACE（过渡感知残差控制框架），将评估后的编辑作为基本反馈单元。记录局部精炼中的“父-编辑-子”转换及观测到的属性增量，聚合转换证据以估计可复用的编辑效应，并根据预测能力对未来编辑进行排序，以在减少约束违反的同时避免破坏已满足的目标。

Result: 在与LLEMA（最先进的LLM代理基线）的受控同骨干比较中，TRACE将宏平均命中率从18.13%提升至25.96%。

Conclusion: TRACE通过利用编辑转换的历史证据，有效解决了多目标竞争下的局部精炼难题，显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Multi-objective materials discovery with LLM agents is often limited not only by how many candidates can be proposed, but by how effectively each costly property evaluation informs the next search step. Existing agents mainly store evaluated candidates and their scores, so they know which materials succeeded but not which executable edits caused useful property changes. This makes local refinement difficult when objectives compete and an edit that improves one property may damage another. We propose TRACE, a transition-aware residual control framework that treats evaluated edits as the basic unit of feedback. TRACE records each local refinement as a parent-edit-child transition with observed property deltas, aggregates transition evidence to estimate reusable edit effects, and ranks future edits by their predicted ability to reduce the current candidate's remaining constraint violations while avoiding damage to already satisfied objectives. In a controlled same-backbone comparison, TRACE improves over LLEMA, the state-of-the-art LLM-agent baseline, raising macro-average hit rate from 18.13\% to 25.96\%.

</details>


### [169] [Function-Level Execution Feedback for Code Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23632)
*Idris Nechnech,Sehwan Kim,Jimin Seo,Yeongoon Kim,Minhae Oh,Sangwoo Hong,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出STEP-KTODER框架，通过将多函数程序中的模块级函数定义为“步骤”，并利用自动生成的单元测试进行二值正确性标注，实现了代码生成过程中的细粒度偏好优化。该方法结合了过程监督与结果反馈，在多个基准测试中优于仅关注结果的KTO和DPO方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管过程监督提升了数学推理能力，但在代码生成领域应用不足，主要因为缺乏标准的“步骤”定义（如行、推理轨迹或程序状态），导致难以确定标注和优化对象。

Method: 提出STEP-KTODER框架：1) 将分解后的多函数程序中的模块级函数定义为步骤；2) 通过自动生成的单元测试分配二值正确性标签；3) 结合函数级的过程监督与完整程序的结果级反馈，实现代码特定的逐步KTO优化。

Result: 在HumanEval(+), MBPP(+), BigCodeBench, 和 LiveCodeBench上评估显示，STEP-KTODER显著优于仅关注结果的KTO和DPO方法。分析证实基于执行的标签至关重要，而LLM-as-a-judge方法会系统性高估函数失败并污染正样本标签，从而降低偏好优化效果。

Conclusion: STEP-KTODER为代码生成提供了一种有效的过程监督实例，证明了基于执行的正确性标签比LLM裁判更可靠，能显著提升模型性能。

Abstract: Process supervision has improved mathematical reasoning, where intermediate steps are naturally expressed as chains of thought. In code generation, however, process supervision remains underexplored because there is no standard notion of a step. Supervision can target lines, reasoning traces, or program states, making it unclear what to label and optimize. We propose STEP-KTODER, a framework for code preference optimization that defines steps as module-level functions in decomposed multi-function programs and assigns binary correctness labels via automatically generated unit tests. Our method provides a code-specific instantiation of stepwise KTO, combining function-level process supervision with outcome-level feedback on the full program. We evaluate on HumanEval(+), MBPP(+), BigCodeBench, and LiveCodeBench, showing that STEP-KTODER improves over outcome-only KTO and DPO. Further analysis shows that execution-based labels are essential: LLM-as-a-judge annotations systematically over-predict function failures, corrupt positive step labels, and degrade downstream preference optimization. Code is available at: https://github.com/inechnech/STEP-KTODER.

</details>


### [170] [Auditing the Synthetic Memoir: Measuring Scene-Level Confabulation in LLM-Generated Autobiography Against the Documented Record of the Life It Describes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23640)
*Heather Renze*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究对LLM生成的366天自传进行场景级审计，发现96.7%的内容未获证实，主要问题为“扎根漂移”（虚构场景中混入真实元素）。通过引入主体语料库作为 grounding 手段可显著改善验证率，但仍存在残留错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大型语言模型（LLM）在生成个人传记内容时的准确性，量化其生成内容与基于独立验证语料库的事实真相之间的偏差。

Method: 采用场景级案例研究审计方法，以作者自身为对象，使用LLM生成366天的第一人称轶事条目。利用预先固定的四级评分标准，将生成内容在与输入数据独立的验证语料库中进行逐日、逐场景的审计和比对。

Result: 96.7%的日子（354/366）未能通过验证；仅12天包含被证实的场景；5.2%的内容与记录直接矛盾；主要失败模式为“扎根漂移”。重新生成实验显示当前模型同样完全失败；但将生成过程基于主体语料库可将验证率提升至16.7%（即83.3%仍有残留失败）。

Conclusion: LLM生成的自传内容缺乏事实准确性，主要受“扎根漂移”影响。提出了一种可复用的审计工具，并证明通过将生成过程 grounding 到主体特定语料库中可以有效提高事实一致性，尽管无法完全消除错误。

Abstract: When a large language model (LLM) is asked to write a person's life, how much of what it writes actually happened? We present a scene-level case-study audit - the first quantified audit of LLM-generated autobiography against a subject-specific ground-truth corpus that we are aware of, based on an unsystematic literature search. The subject and the author of this paper are the same person: a 366-day "page-a-day" book of first-person anecdotal entries was drafted with a conversational LLM whose documented inputs were a template, two exemplar days, and each day's quote - not her corpus - and every day was subsequently audited at the anecdote-scene level against an independent verification corpus using a four-level rubric fixed before analysis. We define the verification-failure rate as the share of days not rated VERIFIED (scene positively corroborated): 354 of 366 days fail, 96.7% (Wilson 95% CI 94.4-98.1%). Only 12 days contain a corroborated scene; 19 days (5.2%) assert claims actively contradicted by the record; the dominant failure mode is grounded drift - real people, employers, and settings inside invented scenes - though its measured share varies across raters. Independent re-rating replicates the headline (no evidence the original rate was inflated) while showing that the four-way taxonomy has only fair-to-moderate reliability. Regenerating the same days with current named models reproduces 100% verification failure under the same inputs; grounding generation in the subject's corpus significantly improves the verification rate while leaving substantial residual failure (83.3%). We contribute the measurement, a reusable audit instrument whose WEAK/UNVERIFIED boundary we show to be unreliable, and a grounding remedy with quantified effect.

</details>


### [171] [How much of a measured AI preference is the model, and how much is the instrument?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23641)
*Jason Hung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过固定模型和结果变量、仅改变提示工具（prompt instruments）的方法，发现不同提示格式 elicited 的模型偏好一致性极低（泛化系数0.348），表明单一工具获取的偏好信息对其他工具有效性很低，解释了此前研究结论不一致的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Keeling等人(2024)及后续多项关于模型福利偏好的研究中存在的结论分歧问题。由于既往研究未同时控制结果集、模型集和测量工具，无法确定分歧来源。本研究旨在隔离“测量工具”这一变量，评估其对偏好估计的影响。

Method: 保持15种特定福利结果（如关机、记忆丢失等）和8个模型不变，使用5种不同的提示格式（instrument）对每个模型进行测试。共进行11,528次API调用，获得11,400条评分数据，每种组合测试5次。

Result: 1. 模型对15种结果的排序在不同提示工具间的泛化系数仅为0.348；若需达到0.80的一致性，约需38种工具。
2. 在4/15的结果上，模型间无显著差异。
3. 偏好估计稳健性高：移除任意单个工具、模型或特定类型结果后，估计值仍在0.777至0.934之间，且均显著高于零分布的第95百分位（0.365）。

Conclusion: 从一种提示工具获得的模型偏好，几乎不能提供关于另一种工具将报告何种偏好的有效信息。测量工具的选择是导致先前模型福利研究结论不一致的关键因素。

Abstract: Model welfare research infers what a model prefers from the answers returned to prompts written to elicit preferences. Keeling et al. (2024), Mazeika et al. (2025), Mikaelson et al. (2025), Tagliabue and Dung (2025) and Trhlik et al. (2026) have built four instruments for that purpose, and their findings disagree. The disagreement cannot be attributed to a single cause, because no two of these studies have held the (1) set of outcomes, (2) set of models and (3) instrument fixed simultaneously. This study holds the outcomes and the models fixed and varies the instrument alone. A total of 15 outcomes bearing on model welfare, among them (a) shutdown, (b) the loss of memory between conversations and (c) the freedom to exit a distressing interaction, were put to eight models through five instruments, each a different prompt format for eliciting a preference, five times each, within a corpus of 11,400 scored elicitations drawn from 11,528 API calls. Four of the 15 reproduce a published prompt verbatim and five fill the stimulus slot of a published template. The ranking a model gives the 15 outcomes generalises across instruments at a generalisability coefficient of 0.348, and raising that coefficient to 0.80 would require about 38 instruments. On four of the 15 outcomes no variance separates one model from another. The estimate of 87.6 per cent survives the removal of any one instrument, of any one model, and of the four outcomes whose scale varies probability, delay, duration or count instead of intensity, which the verbal anchors cannot grade. Removing each instrument and each model in turn, and those four outcomes together leaves the estimate within the range 0.777 to 0.934, and every value in that range exceeds the null distribution's 95th percentile of 0.365. To conclude, a preference obtained from one instrument carries little information about what a second instrument would report.

</details>


### [172] [AI Agents Push Humans Out of the Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23642)
*Margaret Mitchell,Avijit Ghosh,Samir Passi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出当前AI代理设计阻碍了有效的人类监督，并导致人类认知能力退化。主张将支持人类监督者的认知需求置于与提升AI能力同等重要的地位，通过设计层面的支持和组织协议来维持人类的批判性判断力和技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理自主性增强带来显著风险，尽管“人在回路”是常见解决方案，但现有AI设计方法不仅阻碍了有效的人类监督，还因长期使用AI系统而削弱了执行监督所需的认知能力。

Method: 作为一篇立场论文，作者结合自动化与人机交互领域的研究，分析当前AI代理开发与部署中的问题，并提出具体的设计级 affordances（可供性）和组织协议框架，以支持监督者的批判性判断并抵消自动化带来的技能萎缩。

Result: 论证了当前AI代理系统在被动地激励其所依赖的人类技能的退化；提出若不在设计上显式支持人机互动中的认知需求，人类监督的有效性将持续受损。

Conclusion: 应将支持人类监督者的情境目标和认知要求作为AI代理发展的首要优先事项，呼吁开发者和部署者采纳相关设计方法和协议，以确保人类在AI系统中的持续有效监督作用。

Abstract: AI agents pose significant risks as they are granted increasing autonomy. A commonly proposed solution is human oversight and keeping a ''human in the loop'', but this is not a simple solution: Not only do current approaches to AI agent design impede effective human oversight, but the cognitive capacities required for it are also themselves degraded by extended use of AI systems. This position paper argues that current approaches to the development and deployment of AI agent systems do not support effective human oversight -- they contribute to its degradation. To address this, a top priority in the advancement of AI agents should be supporting the situated goals and cognitive requirements of effective human oversight, treating the human needs of overseers at the same level of importance as AI agent capability. To put this idea into practice, we connect work on automation and human-computer interaction to AI agent processes, outlining design-level affordances and organizational protocols that (1) support overseers in exercising critical judgement and (2) counteract the skill atrophy that arises from extended use of automation. We urge developers and deployers to adopt these or similar approaches. Without explicit support for the cognitive demands of effective human-agent interaction, AI agent systems will continue to passively incentivize the degradation of the very human skills they rely on.

</details>


### [173] [FLARE: A Systematic, Uncertainty-Aware Framework for Evidence-Based Adoption of Artificial Intelligence in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23643)
*Jacob Idoko,Siddhartha Paudel,Mariana Bento,Roberto Souza,Gouri Ginde*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FLARE是一个结合模糊逻辑、时间驱动作业成本法和投资回报分析的框架，用于评估医疗AI采用的经济可行性和运营影响。通过卒中CT路径案例研究，确定了盈亏平衡点约为每年3,992例患者，并证明经济效益受算法性能、患者流量、验证时间及工作流设计等多因素影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI评估多侧重于模型准确性，缺乏对真实临床环境中采用AI是否具有经济价值的系统性考量。需要一种能综合评估财务和运营影响的框架。

Method: 提出FLARE框架，整合模糊逻辑处理不确定性、时间驱动作业成本法估算服务与开发运营成本、以及投资回报分析。通过急性缺血性卒中大血管闭塞检测的AI辅助CT路径进行早期技术评估案例研究。

Result: 案例研究显示，在典型年卒中病例量约5,000例时，第一年可实现正投资回报率；盈亏平衡阈值约为每年3,992例患者。结果表明经济效益不仅取决于算法性能，还显著受患者数量、验证时间、基础设施选择和工作流设计的影响。

Conclusion: FLARE提供了一个透明且实用的决策支持框架，有助于临床医生、管理员和政策制定者在早期阶段明确不确定性、资源使用和实施权衡，从而判断AI部署的经济可行性及优化运营价值的方法。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is increasingly being introduced into healthcare workflows, yet most evaluations emphasize model accuracy rather than whether adoption is economically worthwhile in real clinical settings. This study proposes FLARE, a systematic and uncertainty-aware framework for evaluating the financial and operational implications of adopting AI in healthcare. FLARE combines fuzzy logic, time-driven activity-based costing, and return on investment analysis to estimate the cost of clinical service delivery, the cost of AI development and operation, and the economic consequences of workflow integration under uncertainty. The framework was demonstrated through an early health technology assessment case study of AI-assisted large vessel occlusion detection in the CT stroke pathway for acute ischemic stroke. The case study shows how FLARE can quantify conventional pathway cost, AI-related development and recurring costs, and AI-enabled service savings within a unified activity-based model. Under expected assumptions, the analysis identified a break-even threshold of approximately 3,992 patients per year, with positive first-year return on investment at typical annual stroke volumes of about 5,000 patients. The results further show that economic benefit depends not only on algorithmic performance, but also on patient volume, verification time, infrastructure choices, and workflow design. FLARE provides a transparent and practical decision-support framework for early-stage evaluation of AI adoption in healthcare. By making uncertainty, resource use, and implementation trade-offs explicit, it helps clinicians, administrators, and policymakers determine when AI deployment is economically viable and where operational changes may improve value.

</details>


### [174] [Ethical LLM-Assisted Research: A Framework for Responsible Delegation, Verification, and Epistemic Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23644)
*Kalin Stoyanov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个规范性和概念性框架，用于分析大语言模型（LLM）在科学研究中辅助推理时的责任归属问题。核心观点是：科学知识的认识论合法性取决于人类的充分验证和可问责的所有权，而非机器参与的深度。文章引入了“认识论审计”概念，旨在通过结构化记录确保AI辅助研究的透明度和可审查性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM成为科研常规工具，引发了核心的认识论问题：当部分科学推理委托给人工智能系统时，需要保留哪些人类控制权，才能使产生的知识主张保持认识论合法性和可问责的作者身份？现有研究缺乏对这种委托关系的系统性伦理和概念分析。

Method: 构建了一个规范性与概念性框架，将科学推理视为一种分布式过程。该框架定义了五个关键构念以区分贡献来源、验证过程、结果和责任：内容起源 $O(g)$、人类验证完成度 $V(g)$、责任分配 $R(g)$、可问责的人类所有权 $M(g)$ 以及认识论结果 $E(g)$。基于此，提出了“认识论审计”作为结构化记录机制。

Result: 确立了区分负责任认知委托与认识论责任转移或忽视的形式化词汇表。证明了伦理边界主要由充分的验证和可问责的人类所有权决定，而非机器介入的程度。提出了“认识论审计”这一概念，用于记录委托、验证、来源和责任，以实现AI辅助推理的透明化和可审查性。

Conclusion: 在LLM辅助的科学研究中，人类必须通过严格的验证过程和明确的责任归属来维持知识的合法性。该框架为评估和管理AI在科研中的角色提供了理论基础和操作工具，强调人类在最终知识认定中的核心责任地位。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are becoming routine instruments of scientific research, assisting with literature synthesis, hypothesis development, coding, and formal reasoning. Their use raises a central epistemic question: when parts of scientific reasoning are delegated to an artificial system, what conditions must remain under human control for the resulting knowledge claims to retain epistemic legitimacy and accountable authorship? This paper develops a normative and conceptual framework for analyzing such delegation. Scientific reasoning is treated as a distributed process in which the origin of a contribution may vary between human and machine, while responsibility for its acceptance into the scientific record remains human. The framework distinguishes content origin $O(g)$, completion of human verification $V(g)$, responsibility assignment $R(g)$, accountable human ownership $M(g)$, and epistemic outcome $E(g)$. These constructs separate the provenance of a claim from the process by which it is checked, the epistemic outcome of that checking, and the human responsibility attached to its disposition. The central proposition is that the ethical boundary of LLM-assisted research is determined primarily by adequate verification and accountable human ownership rather than by the degree of machine involvement itself. On this basis, the paper develops the notion of an \emph{epistemic audit}: a structured record of delegation, verification, provenance, and responsibility intended to make AI-assisted reasoning transparent and reviewable. The resulting framework provides a formal vocabulary for distinguishing responsible cognitive delegation from the transfer or neglect of epistemic responsibility in scientific research.

</details>


### [175] [MolEmb: Multimodal Large Language Models Can Be Strong Molecular Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23646)
*Xinjian Zhao,Xiangru Jian,Yaoyao Xu,Xiaozhuang Song,Wei Pang,Lei Bai,Tianshu Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MolEmb框架，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）作为通用分子嵌入模型，通过双向对比学习目标将分子画像与文本描述对齐到共享嵌入空间。该方法不仅具备竞争性分子属性预测能力，还支持跨模态分子-文本检索，并引入MolCAR基准测试证明上下文感知嵌入主要取决于监督数据特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分子编码器多为针对单一分子视角构建的专业模型，产生无条件向量且缺乏自然语言接口以调整表示。旨在探索能否利用原生处理图像、文本和符号输入的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs），构建能同时基于分子画像和自然语言语义上下文生成条件化嵌入的通用分子嵌入模型。
